repositions the tooltip element to avoid going off-screen\n- Hook uses `element.getBoundingClientRect()` + DOM offset calculation, no inline `<script>` tags\n\n**Resizer drag** (app.js hook):\n- Hook name: `wire:StreamResizer`\n- Pointer down/move/up event listeners (added via `el.addEventListener`, not inline `onmousedown`)\n- Adjusts `--stream-height` CSS custom property or applies a transform/resize class\n- ArrowUp/Down/Home/End keyboard handling for keyboard users\n\n**Ping animations** (CSS):\n- Expanding-ring animation on new log entries is pure CSS animation + `@keyframes` in the style block\n- No JavaScript driving the animation\n\n**Level filter leitstand shortcut** (phx-click):\n- Level filter pills use `phx-click=\"toggle_level\"` (or equivalent) to send the click to LiveView\n- LiveView handler computes the shortcut logic (all→single, single→all, etc.) and patches the page with new filter state\n\n**War-room banner timer** (app.js hook):\n- Hook updates the elapsed-time text every 1 second via `setInterval`\n- No inline event handlers on the banner itself; the hook attaches listeners on mount\n\n### AC12: E2E Playwright test coverage for the new page and interaction scenarios\n\nA new feature file `e2e/features/admin/logs-trace-map.feature` is created with BDD scenarios covering:\n\n**Scenario 1: Map renders with domains and satellites**\n- Given I'm logged in as an admin\n- When I navigate to `/admin/logs/trace-map`\n- Then I see the Trace Map page title\n- And I see \"nebu\" center node\n- And I see domain nodes for \"Matrix Client\", \"Matrix Media\", \"Admin\", \"Health\"\n- And I see satellite nodes under each domain (e.g., sync, send, messages under Matrix Client)\n\n**Scenario 2: Status colors reflect log entries**\n- Given the Trace Map is loaded with live traffic\n- When I inject a warning log entry for the `/sync` endpoint\n- Then the sync satellite node changes to warn state (amber, ▲ badge)\n- And the Matrix Client domain inherits warn state\n- And nebu inherits warn state\n- When I inject an error for the same endpoint\n- Then the node changes to error state (red, ✕ badge, pulse animation visible)\n\n**Scenario 3: Clicking a node toggles stream filter selection**\n- Given the Trace Map is loaded\n- When I click the \"sync\" satellite node\n- Then the node shows a selection ring (orange)\n- And `aria-pressed=\"true\"` on the node\n- And a filter chip appears in the stream header: \"sync\"\n- And the log stream below shows only entries from `/sync`\n- When I click another node (\"send\")\n- Then both nodes show selection rings\n- And two filter chips appear: \"sync\" and \"send\"\n- And the stream shows entries from BOTH endpoints (OR logic)\n- When I click \"nebu\" center node\n- Then all selection rings disappear\n- And all filter chips are removed\n\n**Scenario 4: Keyboard navigation (Tab, Enter, Space)**\n- Given the Trace Map is loaded\n- When I press Tab\n- Then focus moves to the first node (nebu)\n- And a visible focus ring appears (primary orange)\n- When I press Tab again\n- Then focus moves to the next node (first domain)\n- When I press Enter on a focused domain node\n- Then the node is selected (selection ring, aria-pressed=true)\n- And filter chip appears\n- When I press Space on the same node\n- Then the node is deselected (no ring, aria-pressed=false)\n- And the filter chip is removed\n\n**Scenario 5: Tooltip on hover and focus**\n- Given the Trace Map is loaded\n- When I hover over the \"sync\" node\n- Then a tooltip appears with: name, route, status, counts, CTA text\n- When I move mouse away\n- Then the tooltip disappears\n- When I Tab to the \"sync\" node and it receives focus\n- Then the tooltip appears again\n- When I press Escape (or Tab away)\n- Then the tooltip disappears\n\n**Scenario 6: Inspector panel shows live stats for selected node**\n- Given the Trace Map is loaded\n- When I click the \"sync\" node\n- Then the inspector panel on the right shows:\n  - \"sync\" node name (bold)\n  - Route: `/_matrix/client/v3/sync` (mono)\n  - Status pill: \"Healthy\" (or Warn/Error if there are logs)\n  - \"450 requests in the last 60s\"\n  - \"1 warning in the last 60s\"\n  - \"0 errors in the last 60s\"\n  - Last 5 events (list of timestamp + level + message)\n\n**Scenario 7: War-room banner appears when session is active**\n- Given a war-room session is active (started via the War Room page)\n- When I navigate to the Trace Map page\n- Then a red banner appears at the top with:\n  - Recording dot (animated)\n  - Text: \"War Room session active — capturing @alice:nebu.example · 00:45\"\n  - \"Open War Room\" button\n- When I click \"Open War Room\"\n- Then I'm navigated to `/admin/logs/war-room`\n\n**Scenario 8: Level filter leitstand shortcut**\n- Given the Trace Map is loaded with all level filters active (DEBUG, INFO, WARN, ERROR)\n- When I click the INFO level pill\n- Then only INFO-level logs appear in the stream\n- And only the INFO pill shows as active (colored)\n- When I click INFO again (the sole active level)\n- Then all levels become active again\n- And all level pills appear colored\n\n**Scenario 9: Resizable stream panel (drag handle)**\n- Given the Trace Map is loaded\n- When I drag the resizer handle downward\n- Then the stream panel grows taller\n- And the map shrinks\n- When I drag upward\n- Then the stream shrinks and map grows\n- When I dblclick the resizer\n- Then the stream resets to default height (218px)\n- When I Tab to the resizer and press ArrowUp\n- Then the stream height decreases by 32px\n- When I press ArrowDown\n- Then the stream height increases by 32px\n- When I press Home on the resizer\n- Then the stream is minimized (max height for map)\n- When I press End\n- Then the stream is maximized (min height for map)\n\n**Scenario 10: Bookmarkable URLs with filter state**\n- Given the Trace Map is loaded\n- When I select \"sync\" and \"send\" endpoints via clicking\n- Then the URL updates to: `/admin/logs/trace-map?endpoint[]=%2Fmatrix%2Fclient%2Fv3%2Fsync&endpoint[]=%2Fmatrix%2Fclient%2Fv3%2Fsend`\n- When I copy the URL and reload in a new tab\n- Then the page loads with both nodes pre-selected (rings + chips visible)\n- And the stream is pre-filtered to those endpoints\n\n**Also retiring/rewriting stale specs**:\n- `e2e/features/admin/logs-monitoring.feature`: Scenarios asserting the OLD embedded Trace Map on `/admin/logs` must be updated or deleted (the embedded bubbles/tree are being removed; the Live Stream page keeps level/source filters, not endpoint trace-map)\n- `e2e/features/admin/logs-trace-map-filter-persistence.feature` (if exists): Rewrite assertions to target the new `/admin/logs/trace-map` page, not the old embedded widget\n- `e2e/features/admin/logs-trace-map-hierarchy.feature` (if exists): Same rewrite — the tree structure exists on the new dedicated page (if at all), not embedded\n- Alternative: Delete the old feature files if the new `logs-trace-map.feature` fully supersedes them (simplest for this sprint)\n\n### AC13: Retiring/repurposing embedded Trace Map components\n\nThe Trace Map is being removed from the `/admin/logs` Live Stream page to focus the page on its core function (live stream with level/source filters):\n\n**TraceMapBubble** component:\n- **Keep** if reused elsewhere (e.g., to style legend dots, or for future features)\n- **Delete** if it only served the embedded widget on `/admin/logs`\n- **Dev decision**: Document the choice in Dev Notes (e.g., \"TraceMapBubble retained for legend styling in trace-map-leitstand; removed from logs_live.ex\")\n\n**TraceMapTree** component:\n- Similar: keep if useful, delete if only served the embedded `/admin/logs` widget\n- Note: The tree (hierarchical endpoint grouping) is also being removed from the Live Stream page; the Leitstand page has a simpler flat list of satellites per domain (not a nested disclosure tree)\n\n**Impact on logs_live.ex**:\n- Remove the render section that renders the flat bubble list (Endpoint dimension) and the tree (if it existed)\n- Keep the level/source filter sections (these remain on the Live Stream page)\n- Update the relevant tests to reflect the removal of endpoint filtering from `/admin/logs`\n\n### AC14: Coordination note — UX Gate Advisory A1 (Configuration label duplicate)\n\nThe `admin-sidebar-nesting` story flagged an advisory A1: the Logs sidebar section has a child item **\"Configuration\"** (for log retention settings at `/admin/logs/config`), which duplicates the top-level **Configuration** section.\n\n**Phil's decision (pending 2026-07-07)**:\n- **Option A** (default if no decision yet): Rename the Logs child from \"Configuration\" to \"Log Retention\" (Phil's decision, 2026-07-07), keeping the navkey `logs-config` unchanged. This disambiguates the sections.\n- **Option B**: Drop the Logs child entirely (no `/admin/logs/config` link in the Logs submenu); users access retention settings via the top-level Configuration section instead.\n\n**This story must resolve A1** by:\n- If Option A chosen: Update the Logs submenu's child label (template in `admin-sidebar-nesting`'s layout, or nav data structure) from \"Configuration\" to the renamed label\n- If Option B chosen: Remove the `/admin/logs/config` child item from the Logs submenu (but `/admin/logs/config` route and the retention settings page itself remain; just no nav shortcut under Logs)\n- Document the decision in Dev Notes under \"UX Coordination\"\n\n**Current expectation**: DECIDED by Phil 2026-07-07: the label is \"Log Retention\" (navkey logs-config stays)\n\n## Dev Notes\n\n### Constraints from Spec §8 (Hard Implementation Constraints)\n\n1. **Filter/selection state in LiveView assigns driven by URL query params** — never client-DOM-only state (root cause of trace-map-filter-reset bug):\n   - State lives in `@active_filters` (LiveView assigns), keyed by dimension (e.g., `%{\"endpoint\" => [route1, route2]}`)\n   - On mount, `handle_params/3` parses URL params and seeds state\n   - On filter toggle, `push_patch/1` updates the URL, triggering `handle_params/3` again\n   - Every page load/navigation restores state from the URL\n   - This ensures state survives live traffic, page refreshes, crashes, and bookmarking\n\n2. **CSP is `script-src 'self'`, no inline JS**:\n   - Client-only behavior (tooltip positioning, resizer drag, ping animations) via app.js `phx-hook` pattern or `JS.dispatch` commands\n   - No `<script>` tags in HEEx templates\n   - No inline event handlers (e.g., `onmouseover=\"...\"`)\n\n3. **SVG server-side, positions computed in Elixir**:\n   - A plain Elixir module (e.g., `Nebu.Admin.TraceMapGeometry`) computes (x, y) for each node\n   - SVG is rendered in HEEx with the computed positions (no client-side geometry)\n   - LiveView patches update node classes/counters, not positions\n\n4. **Accessibility is blocking**:\n   - Nodes are focusable `role=\"button\" aria-pressed` elements with status+counts in aria-labels\n   - Aria-live announcements for new errors (throttled)\n   - Visible focus rings; prefers-reduced-motion disables pulse/pings\n   - WCAG AA contrast per Obsidian theme tokens\n   - Playwright tests use `getByRole`/`getByLabel` (accessibility-first)\n\n5. **No sensitive data on the map, tooltips, or stream**:\n   - Route templates and counts only; never payloads, tokens, message content\n   - Inspector shows only endpoint name/route/status/counts, no message bodies\n\n6. **E2E coverage must grow with every UI story** (pipeline CI gate)\n\n### Technical Architecture Notes\n\n**Status Calculation (handle_info/2)**:\n- Every time a new log entry arrives via PubSub, the page's `handle_info({:operational_log_entry, entry}, socket)` handler:\n  1. Extracts the endpoint and level from the entry\n  2. Finds the corresponding node in the map\n  3. Increments the appropriate counter (request, warn, or error) for the 60-second window\n  4. Re-computes the node's status and its ancestors' (domain, nebu)\n  5. Calls `assign(:node_statuses, updated_map)` to trigger a patch\n  6. HEEx renders the updated classes on affected nodes (`:ok`, `:warn`, `:error`)\n\n**Window Management**:\n- Nodes store a rolling buffer of timestamps for each level/count\n- Every 1-5 seconds, a server-side cleanup removes entries older than 60 seconds\n- Alternatively: compute the window on-the-fly from LogStore queries (simpler, but may be slower for large volumes)\n\n**Filter Interaction Flow**:\n1. User clicks node (phx-click handler)\n2. Handler extracts the node key and toggles it in `@active_filters[\"endpoint\"]`\n3. Handler calls `push_patch(path: ..., params: new_filters)` to update URL\n4. `handle_params/3` is triggered with the new URL\n5. Assigns are updated, template re-renders, filter chips + stream update\n6. Live entries arriving during all of this flow are filtered by the current `@active_filters` before being rendered\n\n**Log Stream Filtering**:\n- Entries are filtered by both endpoint selection AND level filter before rendering\n- Filtering logic in `handle_info/2` and/or a helper function (e.g., `apply_filters/2`)\n- Only entries matching the current filter are appended to the stream\n\n### Files to Create/Modify\n\n**Create**:\n- `server/lib/nebu/admin/trace_map_geometry.ex` — Elixir module computing radial SVG positions\n- `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map.ex` (or inline in `logs_live.ex`) — LiveView mount/render for `/admin/logs/trace-map` action\n- `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_components.ex` (optional) — Helper components for map rendering, inspector, legend\n- `server/priv/static/assets/trace-map.hook.js` (optional) — App.js hook for tooltip positioning, resizer drag, timer updates\n\n**Modify**:\n- `server/lib/nebu/web/router.ex` — Add `/admin/logs/trace-map` route\n- `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live.ex` — Extend mount/render to handle `:trace_map` action (or create a separate module)\n- `server/lib/nebu/admin/layouts.ex` (or nav data structure) — Resolve A1 (rename \"Configuration\" child label or drop it)\n- `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live.ex` (live_stream action) — Remove embedded TraceMapBubble/TraceMapTree rendering, keep level/source filters\n- `server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live_test.exs` — Add tests for trace-map-leitstand mount, filtering, status updates\n- `e2e/features/admin/logs-trace-map.feature` — New feature file (scenarios 1-10 above)\n- `e2e/features/admin/logs-monitoring.feature` (if exists) — Delete or rewrite stale embedded trace-map specs\n- `e2e/features/admin/logs-trace-map-filter-persistence.feature` (if exists) — Rewrite for new page, or delete\n- `e2e/features/admin/logs-trace-map-hierarchy.feature` (if exists) — Rewrite for new page, or delete\n\n### Known Challenges & Design Decisions\n\n1. **Window slide-in efficiency**: Computing the 60-second window on every incoming entry could be slow for high-volume logs. Consider:\n   - Option A: Keep a rolling buffer of `{timestamp, level}` tuples per node, prune on a timer\n   - Option B: Query LogStore's database for entries in the window (trades latency for simplicity)\n   - Chosen: **Option A for now** (simple, in-memory, fast); if profiling shows DB is faster, switch to Option B\n\n2. **Legend card always visible**: The inspector toggles between a \"no selection\" message and node details. The legend is below, always visible. Together they form a sticky right-column sidebar. Layout: `flex flex-col gap-X` with inspector taking `flex-1` and legend fixed height at bottom.\n\n3. **A1 resolution pending**: The label rename decision impacts the sidebar template. DECIDED: label is \"Log Retention\" (Phil, 2026-07-07); navkey logs-config unchanged.\n\n### Testing Strategy\n\n**Unit tests** (`logs_live_test.exs`):\n- Mount with no filters → all nodes render\n- Mount with URL params `?endpoint=/sync` → sync node is selected\n- Inject log entry → counters update, status propagates up\n- After 61 seconds, entry ages out → status heals\n- Click node → filter applied, URL updated, re-render\n- Click filtered node again → filter removed, URL updated\n- Click nebu → all filters cleared\n\n**Integration tests** (same file):\n- Full-stack: mount → live traffic → filter → verify stream content matches endpoint selection\n- War-room session active/inactive → banner appears/disappears\n- Level filter shortcut: all active → click one → only one active → click that one → all active again\n\n**E2E tests** (`logs-trace-map.feature`):\n- All 10 scenarios above\n- Keyboard navigation (Tab, Enter, Space, Arrow, Home/End)\n- Resizer interaction (pointer drag, keyboard)\n- Tooltip appearance/disappearance\n- Bookmarkable URLs\n- (Pending follow-up): long-polling off-tier endpoints, temporary capture timer\n\n### Predecessor Story Intelligence\n\n**From `trace-map-filter-reset-bugfix` (completed 2026-07-06)**:\n- Root cause of filter reset: re-renders wiping client-side filter state (`display:none` on rows)\n- Solution: **filter state in URL query params**, not DOM classes\n- Lesson: Use `push_patch` + `handle_params/3`, not client-side `phx-update`\n\n**From `trace-map-hierarchy` (completed 2026-07-06)**:\n- Hierarchical tree rendering pattern (domain parent, endpoint children)\n- `TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint/1` is the canonical domain heuristic\n- Accessibility: `aria-expanded` on disclosure buttons, natural Tab order for nested items\n- Lesson: Reuse existing classification logic; don't reinvent the wheel\n\n**From `admin-sidebar-nesting` (completed 2026-07-07)**:\n- Navigation state: `@active_nav` assign, matched against nav item keys\n- Nested nav pattern: parent disclosure button + child list\n- Lesson: Logs section now has 3 children; Trace Map is one of them; set `@active_nav=\"logs-trace-map\"` to highlight correctly\n\n### Known Gaps (Out of Scope, Follow-up Story)\n\n**`trace-map-node-controls` story** (not this one) will implement:\n- Inspector logging controls (min level select, retention select, cascading to domain/nebu)\n- Long-polling endpoints off by default (gray, dashed, \"off\" text, no logs collected)\n- Temporary capture: click enables 5-minute logging, countdown on node + inspector, auto-fallback to off after timer\n\n### CSP & Security Compliance\n\n- **script-src 'self'**: All client interactivity via phx-hook pattern, no inline scripts\n- **No sensitive data**: Route templates + counts only, never payloads/tokens/message bodies (complies with \"No Sensitive Data in Logs Law\" from MEMORY.md)\n- **No raw user input**: Node names come from hardcoded domain list or observed routes (no XSS surface)\n- **RLS/auth**: Filter state in URL params is readable by the user; filtering is presentation-only (no access-control enforcement on the stream itself — that's handled by LogStore's existing RLS policy)\n\n### Accessibility Compliance Summary\n\n- ✓ Native button elements (role=button) for all interactive nodes\n- ✓ aria-pressed for toggle state\n- ✓ aria-expanded for disclosure (future: domain grouping)\n- ✓ aria-label for all nodes (name + route + status + counts)\n- ✓ aria-live for error announcements\n- ✓ Visible :focus-visible rings on all focusable elements\n- ✓ prefers-reduced-motion disables animations\n- ✓ WCAG AA contrast for all text + status colors\n- ✓ Keyboard: Tab, Enter, Space, Arrow, Home/End\n- ✓ Playwright tests use getByRole/getByLabel\n\n## Questions & Follow-ups\n\nNone at this time — the design spec and dummy are comprehensive and approved by Phil. Implementation guidance is clear; constraints are explicit. Ready to start dev-story cycle.\n\n## Story Amendments (2026-07-07, pipeline test-review)\n\n- **AC5/AC12 URL format:** multi-select filter query params use bracket-array form\n  (`?endpoint[]=X&endpoint[]=Y`) — Plug's query decoding is last-wins on repeated bare\n  keys, so the originally written literal form cannot round-trip. Tests pin the\n  bracket-array contract; this amendment approves it.\n- **AC12 Scenario 4 wording:** \"first Tab focuses the nebu node\" is not achievable by an\n  accessible implementation (sidebar chrome precedes main content in document order).\n  Amended to: focus reaches the nebu node via sequential keyboard navigation (test may\n  use a tab-until-focused loop or programmatic focus helper); focus-ring, Enter and\n  Space assertions unchanged.\n- **AC4 deferral note:** the \"logging off\" and \"temp capture\" visual states are\n  implemented in the follow-up story trace-map-node-controls (no endpoint is off-tier\n  classified by this story); the always-visible legend still enumerates all 6 states.\n- **AC12 log injection:** WARN/ERROR entries for E2E are seeded via direct DB insert\n  into operational_logs (fixture; in-repo raw-SQL precedent from logs_live_test.exs) —\n  NO test-only HTTP routes in server code.\n\n## Dev Agent Record\n\n### Implementation Plan / Completion Notes (2026-07-07, dev-story cycle 0)\n\n- **Three new modules** (per the ATDD-pinned contracts):\n  - `Nebu.Admin.TraceMapGeometry` (plain module, ADR-018/021) — canonical\n    4-domain fixed angles (−48/48/132/−132) with even-distribution fallback\n    starting at −90°; satellite fan `min(130°, n·26°)`; `polar_to_cartesian/2`\n    (SVG convention, no Y-flip); `trim_edge/2` (node radius + 4px per end);\n    `layout/1` (nebu at origin r=52, domains R1=195 r=38, satellites R2=128\n    r=24 relative to their OWN domain). `domain_angles([])` added (empty map\n    on first mount — the 360/0 division the tests didn't cover).\n  - `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapStatus` (pure module) — AC3's 60 s sliding\n    window as pure functions of `(state, now_ms)`; `worst/1` for worst-child\n    propagation; auto-heal falls out of purity (no ack/heal operation exists).\n    `record/3` self-prunes aged events so per-node memory stays bounded with\n    no cleanup timer.\n  - `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap` (LiveView, `/admin/logs/trace-map`,\n    `@active_nav \"logs-trace-map\"`) — a SEPARATE module from `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`\n    (AC1's \"(or alias)\" branch), because it exports `handle_params/3` (AC5's\n    URL-driven filter state), which would break every `live_isolated/3` test\n    of the shared LogsLive module. Selection state: `@selected` seeded from\n    bracket-array `?endpoint[]=…` params in `handle_params/3`; every node\n    click round-trips `toggle_node`/`clear_filters` → `push_patch` →\n    `handle_params/3` (story amendment: bracket-array, not bare repeated keys).\n    Status events recorded per node (satellite + domain + nebu) at ingest;\n    parents' rendered status uses `TraceMapStatus.worst/1`. A 1 s server tick\n    advances `@now_ms` so auto-heal is visible without traffic. War-room\n    status polled every 2 s (both directions — banner appears if a session\n    starts while the page is open), guarded behind `Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo)`\n    like all DB reads (mount stays DB-free for unit tests).\n- **Domain grouping REUSES `TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint/1` /\n  `group_endpoints_by_domain/1`** (hard constraint; never duplicated).\n  Bookmarked selections for not-yet-observed endpoints render as nodes\n  immediately (a key that IS its own domain, e.g. `/admin`, renders as a\n  domain-only node, never a synthetic satellite).\n- **CSP (`script-src 'self'`)**: one `TraceMapLeitstand` phx-hook in\n  `server/priv/static/assets/app.js` owns ALL client-only behavior: Enter/Space\n  activation of SVG nodes (SVG `<g role=\"button\">` has no native keyboard\n  activation — keydown dispatches the same bubbling click phx-click handles),\n  tooltip show/hide/position (content from server-rendered `data-tooltip-*`,\n  built with `textContent` only), resizer (pointer capture drag, ArrowUp/Down\n  ±32, Home/End, dblclick→218px, clamp 64…column−140), stream autoscroll\n  (paused on hover/focus), war-room elapsed clock, decorative status-transition\n  pings (elements created by JS, animation pure CSS). No inline JS/`on*=`\n  anywhere (pinned by the AC11 test).\n- **Visual states** in `server/assets/css/app.css` (`tm-*` classes, Obsidian\n  token values, per the dummy): ok/warn/error node cores, orange-only\n  selection ring + focus-visible outlines, ▲/✕ badges with counts (never\n  color-alone), tooltip, resizer, legend dots for all 6 states (off/temp are\n  legend-only this story, per the amendment), `@keyframes tm-errpulse` /\n  `tm-ping` / `tm-recblink` all disabled under `prefers-reduced-motion`.\n  `make assets` re-ran; new utilities verified in the rebuilt app.css.\n- **AC13 retirement**: `logs_live.ex` dropped the Endpoints section\n  (TraceMapTree render, endpoint chips, `toggle_domain`, `@domain_overrides`,\n  `@endpoint_order`/`@endpoint_counts`, \"endpoint\" from `@filter_dimensions`).\n  Levels/Sources sections unchanged. **Component decision: `TraceMapTree` and\n  `TraceMapBubble` modules are KEPT** — TraceMapBubble still renders the\n  Levels/Sources bubbles (and the logs-config page), TraceMapTree's\n  `domain_for_endpoint/1`/`group_endpoints_by_domain/1` are the canonical\n  domain heuristic the Leitstand reuses (its `endpoint_tree/1` component is\n  now render-unused but its component tests keep passing; flagged for a\n  future cleanup story rather than deleted mid-story).\n- **AC14 (UX advisory A1)**: Logs child label \"Configuration\" → **\"Log\n  Retention\"** (Phil's decision 2026-07-07); navkey `logs-config` and the\n  `/admin/logs/config` route unchanged. No sidebar-nesting test asserted the\n  old label (they pin keys only), so no test updates were needed for this.\n- **Test updates (documented deviations from staged ATDD, all\n  intent-preserving)**:\n  - `trace_map_leitstand_test.exs`: (a) `viewBox` lookup → `viewbox` — Floki's\n    HTML tokenizer (lazy_html) lowercases ALL attribute names on parse, so the\n    staged camelCase lookup was unsatisfiable against ANY rendered output (the\n    template renders the correct camelCase `viewBox`); (b) the \"deactivating\n    WARN\" stream-content test clicked a pill while all 4 levels were active,\n    which per AC9's leitstand shortcut (pinned by the same file's own shortcut\n    tests) isolates that level — the opposite of its assertions; rewritten to\n    click INFO (shortcut-isolate), same assertions unchanged.\n  - `trace_map_geometry_test.exs` / `trace_map_status_test.exs`: `apply/3`\n    RED-phase wrappers converted to direct calls (test-review recommendation).\n  - `logs_live_test.exs` (AC13, count-preserving rewrites — collected total\n    stays 1496): AT1 endpoint-bubble assertions dropped; the URL-seed,\n    push-op, toggle-round-trip, and both popstate/sync_filters tests moved\n    from the endpoint dimension to level/source; the trace-map-hierarchy AT6\n    became \"comma-joined ?level=error,warning multi-select URL seed\"; AT8\n    became the retirement-era zero-regression test (no domain-group/tree\n    markup remains + surviving bubbles still click-to-filter); MINOR-3 became\n    a NEW chip-\"×\"-remove server-round-trip test (covers\n    `handle_event(\"remove_filter\", ...)`, previously untested directly).\n  - E2E: `logs-trace-map-hierarchy.feature` + `logs-trace-map-filter-persistence.feature`\n    (+ steps) DELETED (AC12's \"delete if superseded\" option — the tree\n    interaction pattern is retired outright; filter persistence/URL state is\n    superseded by logs-trace-map.feature Scenarios 3/10 on the new page).\n    The shared \"the page URL contains the query parameter\" step moved into\n    `logs-trace-map.steps.ts`. `logs-monitoring.feature`: Endpoints\n    bubble-section assertion + endpoint-bubble-click scenario removed\n    (Levels/Sources + detail-panel scenarios untouched). `@pending-feature`\n    removed from all 10 logs-trace-map.feature scenarios; `npx bddgen` ok.\n- **Deviation flagged for PM/review — resizer Home/End**: the staged e2e\n  contract (logs-trace-map.feature Scenario 9) pins Home = minimize stream /\n  End = maximize stream; AC9's prose says the opposite. Implementation follows\n  the executable spec (the staged, test-reviewed feature file).\n- **Security notes for Kassandra**: no sensitive data on map/tooltips/labels/\n  announcer (route templates + counts only; the stream/inspector show the\n  same already-redacted `message` field the Live Stream page shows);\n  `toggle_node` accepts arbitrary key strings but they only ever land in the\n  URL/chips as HEEx-escaped text and in presentation-only filtering (same\n  trust model as the pre-existing bookmarkable filter URLs); no new routes\n  outside the `live_session :admin` gate; no test backdoors (E2E WARN/ERROR\n  seeding is a direct DB insert in e2e/fixtures/db.ts, per the amendment).\n- **Verification**: full suite `make test-unit-elixir`: **1496 passed,\n  0 failures** (2 skipped, 368 integration-excluded), `--warnings-as-errors`\n  clean — all 81 previously-red ATDD tests green, zero regressions.\n  `node --check` clean on app.js; `npx bddgen` ok. (No `.formatter.exs`\n  exists in this repo — no format gate to run.)\n\n## File List\n\n- server/lib/nebu/admin/trace_map_geometry.ex (new — AC2 radial geometry, plain module)\n- server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_status.ex (new — AC3 60s-window status model, pure module)\n- server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map.ex (new — the Leitstand LiveView, AC1/AC2/AC4–AC11)\n- server/lib/nebu/admin/router.ex (modified — /logs/trace-map repointed to LogsLive.TraceMap)\n- server/lib/nebu/admin/layouts.ex (modified — AC14 logs-config label \"Log Retention\")\n- server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live.ex (modified — AC13 Endpoints dimension retired; level/source only)\n- server/priv/static/assets/app.js (modified — TraceMapLeitstand phx-hook, AC7/AC9/AC10/AC11)\n- server/assets/css/app.css (modified — tm-* visual states, keyframes, reduced-motion)\n- server/priv/static/assets/app.css (regenerated — make assets)\n- server/test/nebu/admin/trace_map_geometry_test.exs (modified — apply/3 → direct calls)\n- server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_status_test.exs (modified — apply/3 → direct calls)\n- server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_leitstand_test.exs (modified — viewbox lookup fix; cycle 1 — ensureOperationalLogRow, endActiveWarRoomSessions)\n- docs/stories/enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand.md (modified — status/record)\n\n### Dev cycle 1 (2026-07-07) — CI-gate E2E follow-up (1/10 → 10/10 live-stack PASS)\n\nRoot causes found by debugging against the running stack (all fixes are\nimplementation/synchronization — no assertion was weakened):\n\n1. **Recorded traffic invisible to the map (5 scenarios)**: `RequestLogger`\n   logs every hit at INFO, but INFO persistence is policy-gated\n   (`log_collect_info`, default FALSE) — entries are always broadcast but,\n   under the default policy, never written to `operational_logs`. Scenarios\n   record traffic and THEN navigate, so the page's mount-time history read\n   could never see it (the pre-existing logs-monitoring detail-panel\n   scenario had the same latent dependency and also failed on a\n   default-policy stack). Fix: the shared \"a request to {string} has been\n   recorded\" step now makes its own precondition deterministic —\n   `ensureOperationalLogRow` (e2e/fixtures/db.ts) inserts the row the\n   request WOULD have persisted (same real x-request-id/route, INSERT …\n   WHERE NOT EXISTS so an INFO-enabled stack gets no duplicate); same\n   approved DB-seed mechanism as the story amendment's WARN/ERROR seeding.\n2. **Interactions racing the LiveSocket (keyboard/pills/resizer/tooltip)**:\n   the admin UI uses a THROTTLED LONGPOLL transport — the socket connects\n   (and the TraceMapLeitstand hook mounts) a few seconds after first paint;\n   clicks/keys fired before that silently no-op on the static render.\n   Verified interactively that Enter/pills/drag all work once connected.\n   Fix: the hook sets `window.__nebuTraceMapHookReady` in mounted(); every\n   interaction step first awaits it (`awaitLeitstandReady`) — Playwright\n   actionability synchronization, not an assertion change. The same wait\n   (via the generic `[data-phx-main].phx-connected` class) was added to the\n   war-room start step and the logs-monitoring row-click step, whose\n   phx-submit/phx-click had the same race.\n3. **War-room banner \"visible\" while hidden + no session**: (a) the banner\n   div carried the Tailwind `flex` utility, which beats the UA's\n   `[hidden] {display:none}` in the cascade (the known flex-beats-hidden\n   trap) — the \"hidden\" banner still rendered, so toBeVisible passed\n   vacuously and the real failure surfaced as missing text. Fixed with a\n   `.tm-wr-banner` class owning `display:flex` + a `[hidden]` override in\n   app.css. (b) The session itself was never created (the start submit\n   raced the socket — fix 2). (c) The scenario's real session now gets\n   cleaned up via an `After({tags: '@ac6-war-room-banner'})` SQL hook\n   (`endActiveWarRoomSessions`) so war-room.feature's own start can never\n   hit the exactly-one-active-session conflict.\n4. **Focus stability insurance**: map nodes now carry a stable DOM `id`\n   (`tm-node-<base64url(key)>`) so LiveView's DOM patching keys them in\n   place and keyboard focus survives Enter/Space selection patches.\n\nVerified: `logs-trace-map.feature` **10/10 PASS** + rewritten\n`logs-monitoring.feature` **3/3 PASS** (combined 13 passed, 1.1m) and\n`war-room.feature` **6/6 PASS** against the live stack (`make redeploy`ed\nimage); `make test-unit-elixir` **1496 passed, 0 failures** re-confirmed;\n`npx bddgen` ok.\n\nCycle-1 file changes: server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map.ex (node\ndom_id, banner tm-wr-banner class), server/assets/css/app.css +\nserver/priv/static/assets/app.css (tm-wr-banner hidden override),\nserver/priv/static/assets/app.js (hook readiness marker),\ne2e/fixtures/db.ts (ensureOperationalLogRow, endActiveWarRoomSessions),\ne2e/fixtures/nebu-fixtures.ts (export After),\ne2e/step-definitions/admin/logs-trace-map.steps.ts (awaitLeitstandReady +\nAfter cleanup hook), e2e/step-definitions/admin/logs-monitoring.steps.ts\n(deterministic seed + row-click sync),\ne2e/step-definitions/admin/war-room.steps.ts (start-step sync).\n\n## Change Log\n\n- 2026-07-07 (dev-story cycle 0): Implemented the Trace Map Leitstand — radial\n  SVG health map at /admin/logs/trace-map (server-side geometry, 60 s sliding\n  status window with worst-child propagation + auto-heal, URL-driven endpoint\n  multi-select via push_patch/handle_params, war-room banner, tooltip data,\n  inspector + always-visible 6-state legend, resizable level-filtered live\n  stream with leitstand shortcut, throttled aria-live error announcer);\n  retired the embedded Endpoints dimension from /admin/logs (AC13); renamed\n  the Logs sidebar child to \"Log Retention\" (AC14). {"parentUuid":"80b93e01-7492-4e41-9ce6-df6557b495c1","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c1e00925-b2e9-4f38-87d9-fd1f315ab15a","agentId":"aa2a2c647028b9c53","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01RkG7yNCH8RwgDS4xBKfKLd","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Trace Map \"Leitstand\" — Design Spec (2026-07-07)\n2\t\n3\t**Status:** Approved by Phil (\"das ist echt cool\") — authoritative design source for the\n4\tTrace Map redesign and Logs IA restructuring in the `enhanced-logging` epic.\n5\t\n6\t**Source artifacts (all in this directory):**\n7\t- `trace-map-leitstand-dummy.html` — interactive, browser-verified design dummy\n8\t  (open locally or at https://claude.ai/code/artifact/a4d6fd3a-7897-451c-8d52-6fabd30ede9b).\n9\t  The dummy IS the spec for visual treatment, copy, and interaction feel; this document\n10\t  adds the contract-level details and implementation constraints.\n11\t- Screenshots: `tracemap-initial.png`, `tracemap-outage.png`, `tracemap-v2-capture.png`,\n12\t  `tracemap-v2-banner.png`, `tracemap-v3-resized-errorfilter.png`, `warroom-active.png`.\n13\t\n14\t**Why (Phil's verdict on the shipped UX):** the flat bubble list and the disclosure tree\n15\t(`trace-map-hierarchy`) are \"unbrauchbar\" as a monitoring surface. The Trace Map becomes a\n16\tcontrol-room (\"Leitstand\") health map: at a glance, see *where* it is red.\n17\t\n18\t---\n19\t\n20\t## 1. Information architecture\n21\t\n22\tSidebar gets collapsible groups (covered by existing story `admin-sidebar-nesting`):\n23\t\n24\t```\n25\tLogs                 (disclosure group, aria-expanded)\n26\t├── Live Stream      /admin/logs            (existing page, unchanged)\n27\t├── Trace Map        /admin/logs/trace-map  (NEW page — this spec)\n28\t└── War Room         /admin/logs/war-room   (story: war-room-dedicated-page)\n29\tConfiguration        (disclosure group)\n30\t├── General          /admin/config\n31\t├── Role Mapping     /admin/config/role-mapping\n32\t└── Claim Mapping    /admin/config/claim-mapping\n33\t```\n34\t\n35\tThe Trace Map is no longer a filter widget embedded in the logs page — it is its own\n36\tLiveView. The flat `TraceMapBubble` strip and the `TraceMapTree` disclosure tree are\n37\tretired from the logs page in favor of this view (Live Stream keeps its own simpler\n38\tfilters; exact migration is a story-scoping decision).\n39\t\n40\t## 2. The radial map\n41\t\n42\t**Hierarchy:** center `nebu` → domain circles → endpoint satellites.\n43\tDomains come from the existing heuristic `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint/1`\n44\t(`/_matrix/client`, `/_matrix/media`, `/admin`, `/health`, catch-all) — REUSE it, do not\n45\tduplicate. Satellites are the observed `route_template`s, first-observed order.\n46\t\n47\t**Geometry (from the dummy, SVG viewBox −560 −400 1120 800):** center r=52 at origin;\n48\tdomains r=38 on a ring R1≈195 at fixed angles (−48°, 48°, 132°, −132° for 4 domains —\n49\tdistribute evenly if domain count differs); satellites r=24 on R2≈128 around their domain,\n50\tfanned over `min(130°, n·26°)` centered on the domain's outward angle. Edges are trimmed\n51\tlines (node edge + 4px), `--line` color, opacity ~.55.\n52\t\n53\t**Status model:** per node, a sliding **60 s** window over its log events.\n54\t`error > 0 → error`, else `warn > 0 → warn`, else `ok`. A parent's status is the worst\n55\tof its children (satellite → domain → nebu). Nodes heal automatically when the window\n56\tpasses — no manual acknowledge.\n57\t\n58\t**Visual states (Obsidian theme tokens from `server/assets/tailwind.config.js`):**\n59\t\n60\t| State | Fill | Stroke | Extras |\n61\t|---|---|---|---|\n62\t| ok | `base-200` @ 35% opacity | `success` @ 55%, 1.5px | label dimmed — healthy recedes |\n63\t| warn | `#431407` (warn-bg) | `warning`, 2.5px + soft glow | ▲ badge with warn count |\n64\t| error | `#450a0a` (err-bg) | `error`, 3px + glow + pulse animation | ✕ badge with error count |\n65\t| selected | — | extra `primary` ring at r+7, 2.5px | orange = selection ONLY |\n66\t| logging off | `base-200` @ 15% | `text-disabled`, 1.5px **dashed** | count shows \"off\" |\n67\t| temp capture | as status | status color, **dashed** | countdown mm:ss on the node |\n68\t\n69\tStatus colors are reserved for health, primary orange for selection — never cross-used.\n70\tStatus is never conveyed by color alone: badges (▲/✕ + count), \"off\" text, aria-labels.\n71\tCounters inside nodes (requests/60 s; center shows `N/min`) in mono font. Event \"pings\"\n72\t(expanding rings) on nodes are decorative and disabled under `prefers-reduced-motion`\n73\t(as is the error pulse).\n74\t\n75\t## 3. Interactions\n76\t\n77\t- **Click / Enter / Space on a node** toggles it as a stream filter (multi-select, OR\n78\t  within the dimension; domain matches all its satellites). Click on `nebu` clears all\n79\t  filters. Active filters render as removable chips in the stream header + \"Clear all\".\n80\t- **Hover / focus** shows a tooltip (name, route, status, req/warn/err per 60 s, CTA).\n81\t  Tooltip hides on click. Focus also targets the node in the inspector.\n82\t- **Inspector panel (right column)** for the focused/clicked node — see §4.\n83\t- **Legend card** (right column) explains all six states; always visible.\n84\t\n85\t## 4. Inspector = per-node logging control panel\n86\t\n87\tLive stats (requests / warnings / errors per 60 s, status pill, last 5 events) PLUS\n88\tlogging controls:\n89\t\n90\t- **Min level** select (DEBUG/INFO/WARN/ERROR) — wires to the per-endpoint collection\n91\t  level from `admin-logs-retention-settings` (done). On a domain node or `nebu` the\n92\t  setting cascades to all descendant endpoints, with the note \"Applies to all endpoints\n93\t  in this domain\" / \"on the map\".\n94\t- **Retention** select (1 h / 24 h / 7 d / 30 d) — same config backend.\n95\t- **Capture control** for long-polling endpoints (§5): state line + Enable/Disable button\n96\t  with live countdown.\n97\t\n98\tUI contract: the panel is BUILT once per inspected node and only its stat values update\n99\tper tick — selects must never lose value/focus to a re-render (same class of bug as the\n100\ttrace-map-filter-reset incident).\n101\t\n102\t## 5. Long-polling endpoints: off by default, temporary capture\n103\t\n104\tSupersedes the UI concept of `noise-endpoint-bucketing` (ready-for-dev — realign that\n105\tstory): instead of a \"noise bucket\", low-signal long-polling endpoints (default list:\n106\t`/_matrix/client/v3/sync`, `/admin/keepalive`, `/health`; config-driven) are\n107\t**logging-off by default**:\n108\t\n109\t- Rendered gray + dashed, count \"off\", no log data collected, no status.\n110\t- **Click enables temporary capture for 5 minutes** (announce via aria-live): node comes\n111\t  alive with dashed status stroke + countdown on the node and in the inspector;\n112\t  \"Disable now\" in the inspector ends it early. After expiry it falls back to off\n113\t  automatically — server-side timer, no client state.\n114\t- While capturing, a second click toggles the stream filter as normal.\n115\t\n116\t## 6. War-room visibility\n117\t\n118\tWhile a war-room session is active (from `war-room-dedicated-page` / existing\n119\t`war_room_manager.ex`): a red banner across the top of the Trace Map view — recording\n120\tdot + \"War Room session active — capturing {user} · {elapsed}\" + \"Open War Room\" button.\n121\t(Nav-item dot and topbar pill belong to the war-room story; the banner belongs to the\n122\tTrace Map story.)\n123\t\n124\t## 7. Live stream panel (bottom of the Trace Map view)\n125\t\n126\t- Compact mono log lines: time, level (colored), route, message. Autoscroll pauses on\n127\t  hover. Filtered by the map selection AND the level filter.\n128\t- **Resizable:** drag handle between map and stream (`role=\"separator\"`,\n129\t  `aria-orientation=\"horizontal\"`, tabindex=0; pointer drag with capture, ArrowUp/Down\n130\t  ±32px, dblclick resets to default 218px, Home/End max/min; clamp ~64px…viewport−140px).\n131\t- **Level filter pills** (DEBUG/INFO/WARN/ERROR, `aria-pressed`, level colors) with the\n132\t  leitstand shortcut: when ALL levels are active, clicking one shows ONLY that level;\n133\t  clicking the sole active level restores all; otherwise plain toggle.\n134\t\n135\t## 8. Hard implementation constraints\n136\t\n137\t1. **Filter/selection/expansion state lives in LiveView assigns driven by URL query\n138\t   params** (`push_patch` + `handle_params/3`) — NEVER client-DOM-only state on nodes\n139\t   that re-render under live traffic (root cause of the trace-map-filter-reset bug).\n140\t   Bookmarkable URLs fall out for free.\n141\t2. **CSP is `script-src 'self'`, no inline JS.** Client-only behavior (tooltip\n142\t   positioning, resizer drag, ping animations) goes through the established\n143\t   `app.js` `wire*` / `JS.dispatch` pattern or a `phx-hook` defined in\n144\t   `server/priv/static/assets/app.js`.\n145\t3. SVG is rendered server-side in HEEx (positions computed in Elixir — plain module,\n146\t   ADR-018/021 compliant); LiveView patches update node classes/counters.\n147\t4. Accessibility is blocking, not advisory: nodes are focusable\n148\t   `role=\"button\" aria-pressed` elements with status+counts in `aria-label`;\n149\t   `aria-live=\"polite\"` announcements for new errors (throttled) and capture\n150\t   enable/disable; visible focus rings; WCAG AA contrast per the Obsidian tokens;\n151\t   Playwright tests use `getByRole`/`getByLabel`.\n152\t5. No sensitive data on the map, in tooltips, or in the stream (No-Sensitive-Data law —\n153\t   route templates and counts only, never payloads/tokens/message content).\n154\t6. E2E coverage must grow with every UI story (pipeline CI gate).\n155\t\n156\t## 9. Suggested story cut\n157\t\n158\t1. `admin-sidebar-nesting` (exists, ready-for-dev) — matches the dummy as-is.\n159\t2. `war-room-dedicated-page` (exists, backlog) — add ACs: nav-item recording dot,\n160\t   topbar recording pill with elapsed time.\n161\t3. **NEW `trace-map-leitstand`** — the radial map page: hierarchy, status model +\n162\t   propagation, visual states, node filters + chips, tooltip, legend, war-room banner,\n163\t   resizable stream + level filter. (Largest story; may split map vs. stream panel.)\n164\t4. **NEW `trace-map-node-controls`** — inspector logging controls (level/retention wiring,\n165\t   cascade) + long-polling off-by-default with temporary capture (realigns\n166\t   `noise-endpoint-bucketing`).\n167\t\n168\t## Addendum (2026-07-07, Phil)\n169\t- §1 IA correction: the Logs group child pointing at /admin/logs/config is labeled\n170\t  **\"Log Retention\"** (not \"Configuration\"; navkey `logs-config` stays). Resolves UX\n171\t  advisory A1 (duplicate accessible name vs. the top-level Configuration group).\n172\t"}]},"uuid":"d640ad2d-834f-47a0-a183-5cb4746a0088","timestamp":"2026-07-07T17:12:25.900Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/_bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-spec.md","content":"# Trace Map \"Leitstand\" — Design Spec (2026-07-07)\n\n**Status:** Approved by Phil (\"das ist echt cool\") — authoritative design source for the\nTrace Map redesign and Logs IA restructuring in the `enhanced-logging` epic.\n\n**Source artifacts (all in this directory):**\n- `trace-map-leitstand-dummy.html` — interactive, browser-verified design dummy\n  (open locally or at https://claude.ai/code/artifact/a4d6fd3a-7897-451c-8d52-6fabd30ede9b).\n  The dummy IS the spec for visual treatment, copy, and interaction feel; this document\n  adds the contract-level details and implementation constraints.\n- Screenshots: `tracemap-initial.png`, `tracemap-outage.png`, `tracemap-v2-capture.png`,\n  `tracemap-v2-banner.png`, `tracemap-v3-resized-errorfilter.png`, `warroom-active.png`.\n\n**Why (Phil's verdict on the shipped UX):** the flat bubble list and the disclosure tree\n(`trace-map-hierarchy`) are \"unbrauchbar\" as a monitoring surface. The Trace Map becomes a\ncontrol-room (\"Leitstand\") health map: at a glance, see *where* it is red.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Information architecture\n\nSidebar gets collapsible groups (covered by existing story `admin-sidebar-nesting`):\n\n```\nLogs                 (disclosure group, aria-expanded)\n├── Live Stream      /admin/logs            (existing page, unchanged)\n├── Trace Map        /admin/logs/trace-map  (NEW page — this spec)\n└── War Room         /admin/logs/war-room   (story: war-room-dedicated-page)\nConfiguration        (disclosure group)\n├── General          /admin/config\n├── Role Mapping     /admin/config/role-mapping\n└── Claim Mapping    /admin/config/claim-mapping\n```\n\nThe Trace Map is no longer a filter widget embedded in the logs page — it is its own\nLiveView. The flat `TraceMapBubble` strip and the `TraceMapTree` disclosure tree are\nretired from the logs page in favor of this view (Live Stream keeps its own simpler\nfilters; exact migration is a story-scoping decision).\n\n## 2. The radial map\n\n**Hierarchy:** center `nebu` → domain circles → endpoint satellites.\nDomains come from the existing heuristic `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint/1`\n(`/_matrix/client`, `/_matrix/media`, `/admin`, `/health`, catch-all) — REUSE it, do not\nduplicate. Satellites are the observed `route_template`s, first-observed order.\n\n**Geometry (from the dummy, SVG viewBox −560 −400 1120 800):** center r=52 at origin;\ndomains r=38 on a ring R1≈195 at fixed angles (−48°, 48°, 132°, −132° for 4 domains —\ndistribute evenly if domain count differs); satellites r=24 on R2≈128 around their domain,\nfanned over `min(130°, n·26°)` centered on the domain's outward angle. Edges are trimmed\nlines (node edge + 4px), `--line` color, opacity ~.55.\n\n**Status model:** per node, a sliding **60 s** window over its log events.\n`error > 0 → error`, else `warn > 0 → warn`, else `ok`. A parent's status is the worst\nof its children (satellite → domain → nebu). Nodes heal automatically when the window\npasses — no manual acknowledge.\n\n**Visual states (Obsidian theme tokens from `server/assets/tailwind.config.js`):**\n\n| State | Fill | Stroke | Extras |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| ok | `base-200` @ 35% opacity | `success` @ 55%, 1.5px | label dimmed — healthy recedes |\n| warn | `#431407` (warn-bg) | `warning`, 2.5px + soft glow | ▲ badge with warn count |\n| error | `#450a0a` (err-bg) | `error`, 3px + glow + pulse animation | ✕ badge with error count |\n| selected | — | extra `primary` ring at r+7, 2.5px | orange = selection ONLY |\n| logging off | `base-200` @ 15% | `text-disabled`, 1.5px **dashed** | count shows \"off\" |\n| temp capture | as status | status color, **dashed** | countdown mm:ss on the node |\n\nStatus colors are reserved for health, primary orange for selection — never cross-used.\nStatus is never conveyed by color alone: badges (▲/✕ + count), \"off\" text, aria-labels.\nCounters inside nodes (requests/60 s; center shows `N/min`) in mono font. Event \"pings\"\n(expanding rings) on nodes are decorative and disabled under `prefers-reduced-motion`\n(as is the error pulse).\n\n## 3. Interactions\n\n- **Click / Enter / Space on a node** toggles it as a stream filter (multi-select, OR\n  within the dimension; domain matches all its satellites). Click on `nebu` clears all\n  filters. Active filters render as removable chips in the stream header + \"Clear all\".\n- **Hover / focus** shows a tooltip (name, route, status, req/warn/err per 60 s, CTA).\n  Tooltip hides on click. Focus also targets the node in the inspector.\n- **Inspector panel (right column)** for the focused/clicked node — see §4.\n- **Legend card** (right column) explains all six states; always visible.\n\n## 4. Inspector = per-node logging control panel\n\nLive stats (requests / warnings / errors per 60 s, status pill, last 5 events) PLUS\nlogging controls:\n\n- **Min level** select (DEBUG/INFO/WARN/ERROR) — wires to the per-endpoint collection\n  level from `admin-logs-retention-settings` (done). On a domain node or `nebu` the\n  setting cascades to all descendant endpoints, with the note \"Applies to all endpoints\n  in this domain\" / \"on the map\".\n- **Retention** select (1 h / 24 h / 7 d / 30 d) — same config backend.\n- **Capture control** for long-polling endpoints (§5): state line + Enable/Disable button\n  with live countdown.\n\nUI contract: the panel is BUILT once per inspected node and only its stat values update\nper tick — selects must never lose value/focus to a re-render (same class of bug as the\ntrace-map-filter-reset incident).\n\n## 5. Long-polling endpoints: off by default, temporary capture\n\nSupersedes the UI concept of `noise-endpoint-bucketing` (ready-for-dev — realign that\nstory): instead of a \"noise bucket\", low-signal long-polling endpoints (default list:\n`/_matrix/client/v3/sync`, `/admin/keepalive`, `/health`; config-driven) are\n**logging-off by default**:\n\n- Rendered gray + dashed, count \"off\", no log data collected, no status.\n- **Click enables temporary capture for 5 minutes** (announce via aria-live): node comes\n  alive with dashed status stroke + countdown on the node and in the inspector;\n  \"Disable now\" in the inspector ends it early. After expiry it falls back to off\n  automatically — server-side timer, no client state.\n- While capturing, a second click toggles the stream filter as normal.\n\n## 6. War-room visibility\n\nWhile a war-room session is active (from `war-room-dedicated-page` / existing\n`war_room_manager.ex`): a red banner across the top of the Trace Map view — recording\ndot + \"War Room session active — capturing {user} · {elapsed}\" + \"Open War Room\" button.\n(Nav-item dot and topbar pill belong to the war-room story; the banner belongs to the\nTrace Map story.)\n\n## 7. Live stream panel (bottom of the Trace Map view)\n\n- Compact mono log lines: time, level (colored), route, message. Autoscroll pauses on\n  hover. Filtered by the map selection AND the level filter.\n- **Resizable:** drag handle between map and stream (`role=\"separator\"`,\n  `aria-orientation=\"horizontal\"`, tabindex=0; pointer drag with capture, ArrowUp/Down\n  ±32px, dblclick resets to default 218px, Home/End max/min; clamp ~64px…viewport−140px).\n- **Level filter pills** (DEBUG/INFO/WARN/ERROR, `aria-pressed`, level colors) with the\n  leitstand shortcut: when ALL levels are active, clicking one shows ONLY that level;\n  clicking the sole active level restores all; otherwise plain toggle.\n\n## 8. Hard implementation constraints\n\n1. **Filter/selection/expansion state lives in LiveView assigns driven by URL query\n   params** (`push_patch` + `handle_params/3`) — NEVER client-DOM-only state on nodes\n   that re-render under live traffic (root cause of the trace-map-filter-reset bug).\n   Bookmarkable URLs fall out for free.\n2. **CSP is `script-src 'self'`, no inline JS.** Client-only behavior (tooltip\n   positioning, resizer drag, ping animations) goes through the established\n   `app.js` `wire*` / `JS.dispatch` pattern or a `phx-hook` defined in\n   `server/priv/static/assets/app.js`.\n3. SVG is rendered server-side in HEEx (positions computed in Elixir — plain module,\n   ADR-018/021 compliant); LiveView patches update node classes/counters.\n4. Accessibility is blocking, not advisory: nodes are focusable\n   `role=\"button\" aria-pressed` elements with status+counts in `aria-label`;\n   `aria-live=\"polite\"` announcements for new errors (throttled) and capture\n   enable/disable; visible focus rings; WCAG AA contrast per the Obsidian tokens;\n   Playwright tests use `getByRole`/`getByLabel`.\n5. No sensitive data on the map, in tooltips, or in the stream (No-Sensitive-Data law —\n   route templates and counts only, never payloads/tokens/message content).\n6. E2E coverage must grow with every UI story (pipeline CI gate).\n\n## 9. Suggested story cut\n\n1. `admin-sidebar-nesting` (exists, ready-for-dev) — matches the dummy as-is.\n2. `war-room-dedicated-page` (exists, backlog) — add ACs: nav-item recording dot,\n   topbar recording pill with elapsed time.\n3. **NEW `trace-map-leitstand`** — the radial map page: hierarchy, status model +\n   propagation, visual states, node filters + chips, tooltip, legend, war-room banner,\n   resizable stream + level filter. (Largest story; may split map vs. stream panel.)\n4. **NEW `trace-map-node-controls`** — inspector logging controls (level/retention wiring,\n   cascade) + long-polling off-by-default with temporary capture (realigns\n   `noise-endpoint-bucketing`).\n\n## Addendum (2026-07-07, Phil)\n- §1 IA correction: the Logs group child pointing at /admin/logs/config is labeled\n  **\"Log Retention\"** (not \"Configuration\"; navkey `logs-config` stays). Resolves UX\n  advisory A1 (duplicate accessible name vs. the top-level Configuration group).\n","numLines":172,"startLine":1,"totalLines":172}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"80b93e01-7492-4e41-9ce6-df6557b495c1","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

{"parentUuid":"7befd1dd-b9c0-4b93-a13c-07d2432beb36","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c1e00925-b2e9-4f38-87d9-fd1f315ab15a","agentId":"aa2a2c647028b9c53","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013KkG9jXEE6vqCbTLqkinio","type":"tool_result","content":"1\tdefmodule Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap do\n2\t  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n3\t  Trace Map \"Leitstand\" — the dedicated, full-page radial health map at\n4\t  `/admin/logs/trace-map` (enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand).\n5\t\n6\t  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand.md\n7\t  Design: _bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-spec.md\n8\t  Visual reference: trace-map-leitstand-dummy.html (same directory).\n9\t\n10\t  ## Why this is a SEPARATE LiveView module, not a new action on `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`\n11\t\n12\t  AC5 hard-requires URL-driven filter state via `push_patch/2` +\n13\t  `handle_params/3` (the lesson of trace-map-filter-reset-bugfix: filter\n14\t  state must live in LiveView assigns seeded from the URL, NEVER in\n15\t  client-DOM-only state). Merely EXPORTING a `handle_params/3` callback on a\n16\t  LiveView module makes every `live_isolated/3`-mounted test of that module\n17\t  fail at mount (empirically verified against the installed\n18\t  `phoenix_live_view` — see `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s own moduledoc). The\n19\t  existing `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive` (Live Stream page) has a large,\n20\t  deliberately-`live_isolated/3`-based suite, so the Leitstand gets its own\n21\t  module and `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive` stays `handle_params/3`-free.\n22\t\n23\t  ## State model\n24\t\n25\t    * `@selected` — the active endpoint filters (satellite routes and/or\n26\t      domain keys), URL-driven: `handle_params/3` seeds it from the\n27\t      bracket-array `?endpoint[]=X&endpoint[]=Y` query params; every node\n28\t      click round-trips through `handle_event(\"toggle_node\"/\"clear_filters\")`\n29\t      -> `push_patch/2` -> `handle_params/3`. Bracket-array (not the story's\n30\t      literal repeated bare `?endpoint=X&endpoint=Y`) per the story's own\n31\t      \"Story Amendments\": `Plug.Conn.Query.decode/1` is last-wins on repeated\n32\t      bare keys, so the bare form cannot round-trip.\n33\t    * `@node_states` — one `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapStatus` state per map\n34\t      node (`\"nebu\"`, each domain key, each satellite route): the AC3\n35\t      60-second sliding window. Events are recorded at ALL THREE levels on\n36\t      ingest so every node carries its own windowed request/warning/error\n37\t      counts; a parent's STATUS is still derived via\n38\t      `TraceMapStatus.worst/1` over its children (worst-child propagation,\n39\t      spec §2), with the parent's own aggregated state included defensively\n40\t      (covers nil-route entries that belong to no domain, for nebu).\n41\t    * `@active_levels` — the stream panel's level filter (AC9), plain\n42\t      assigns state (the leitstand shortcut is computed server-side in\n43\t      `handle_event(\"toggle_level\", ...)`).\n44\t    * Geometry is computed server-side by `Nebu.Admin.TraceMapGeometry`\n45\t      (spec §8 constraint 3); domain grouping REUSES\n46\t      `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint/1` via\n47\t      `group_endpoints_by_domain/1` (never duplicated).\n48\t\n49\t  ## PubSub / data source\n50\t\n51\t  Same source as the Live Stream page: mount-time history from\n52\t  `Nebu.Web.LogStore.get_logs/1` (guarded behind `Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo)`\n53\t  so plain unit-test runs stay DB-free) + live `\"operational_logs\"` PubSub\n54\t  broadcasts of bare entry maps.\n55\t\n56\t  ## No sensitive data (spec §8 constraint 5)\n57\t\n58\t  The map, tooltips, aria-labels and the live announcer only ever carry\n59\t  route templates + counts. Log MESSAGE text appears only in the stream\n60\t  panel and the inspector's recent-events list — the same already-redacted\n61\t  `message` field the Live Stream page shows (LogStore's writer owns\n62\t  redaction) — never in tooltips/labels/announcements.\n63\t\n64\t  ## CSP (spec §8 constraint 2)\n65\t\n66\t  No inline JS anywhere. Client-only behavior (tooltip positioning, resizer\n67\t  drag/keyboard, Enter/Space activation of SVG nodes, stream autoscroll,\n68\t  war-room elapsed clock, status-transition pings) lives in the\n69\t  `TraceMapLeitstand` phx-hook in `server/priv/static/assets/app.js`;\n70\t  animations (error pulse, recording dot, pings) are pure CSS `@keyframes`\n71\t  in `server/assets/css/app.css`, disabled under `prefers-reduced-motion`.\n72\t  \"\"\"\n73\t  use Phoenix.LiveView, layout: {Nebu.Admin.Layouts, :live}\n74\t\n75\t  alias Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapStatus\n76\t  alias Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapTree\n77\t  alias Nebu.Admin.TraceMapGeometry\n78\t\n79\t  @topic \"operational_logs\"\n80\t  @base_path \"/admin/logs/trace-map\"\n81\t\n82\t  # UI level-pill vocabulary (spec §7) <-> LogStore level strings. The pill\n83\t  # labels are the spec's abbreviated forms; `Nebu.Web.LogStore` entries use\n84\t  # \"warning\"/\"error\" — mapped via ui_level/1 below.\n85\t  @ui_levels [\"DEBUG\", \"INFO\", \"WARN\", \"ERROR\"]\n86\t\n87\t  @domain_labels %{\n88\t    \"/_matrix/client\" => \"Matrix Client\",\n89\t    \"/_matrix/media\" => \"Matrix Media\",\n90\t    \"/admin\" => \"Admin\",\n91\t    \"/health\" => \"Health\"\n92\t  }\n93\t\n94\t  # Bounded in-memory buffers (per-LiveView-process).\n95\t  @max_stream_lines 250\n96\t  @max_inspector_events 5\n97\t  @history_seed_cap 200\n98\t\n99\t  @tick_interval_ms 1_000\n100\t  @war_room_refresh_interval_ms 2_000\n101\t  @announce_debounce_ms 2_000\n102\t\n103\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n104\t  # Mount / handle_params\n105\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n106\t\n107\t  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n108\t  def mount(_params, _session, socket) do\n109\t    Phoenix.PubSub.subscribe(Nebu.PubSub, @topic)\n110\t\n111\t    socket =\n112\t      socket\n113\t      # admin-sidebar-nesting coordination (AC1 / UX gate advisory A2): the\n114\t      # Leitstand highlights the \"Trace Map\" CHILD of the nested Logs section.\n115\t      |> assign(:active_nav, \"logs-trace-map\")\n116\t      |> assign(:page_title, \"Trace Map\")\n117\t      |> assign(:endpoint_order, [])\n118\t      |> assign(:extra_domains, [])\n119\t      |> assign(:node_states, %{})\n120\t      |> assign(:node_events, %{})\n121\t      |> assign(:stream_entries, [])\n122\t      |> assign(:selected, [])\n123\t      |> assign(:active_levels, MapSet.new(@ui_levels))\n124\t      |> assign(:inspected, nil)\n125\t      |> assign(:war_room, initial_war_room_status())\n126\t      |> assign(:announcement, \"\")\n127\t      |> assign(:last_announce_ms, 0)\n128\t      |> assign(:now_ms, System.system_time(:millisecond))\n129\t\n130\t    socket =\n131\t      Enum.reduce(initial_entries(), socket, fn entry, acc ->\n132\t        ingest_entry(acc, entry, announce: false)\n133\t      end)\n134\t\n135\t    if connected?(socket) do\n136\t      schedule_tick()\n137\t      # `WarRoomManager.status/0` is a live DB read — only poll when\n138\t      # `Nebu.Repo` is running (mirrors `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s guard).\n139\t      if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo), do: schedule_war_room_refresh()\n140\t    end\n141\t\n142\t    {:ok, socket}\n143\t  end\n144\t\n145\t  # AC5: URL query params are the single source of truth for the endpoint\n146\t  # selection — seeded here on mount AND after every push_patch round trip.\n147\t  # A bookmarked URL may select endpoints/domains no traffic has been\n148\t  # observed for yet: those are added to the map (as satellite or\n149\t  # domain-only nodes) so the restored view renders its selection rings and\n150\t  # chips immediately (bookmarkable-URL contract, AC5/AC12 Scenario 10).\n151\t  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n152\t  def handle_params(params, _uri, socket) do\n153\t    selected = params |> Map.get(\"endpoint\") |> normalize_selected()\n154\t\n155\t    {endpoint_order, extra_domains} =\n156\t      Enum.reduce(selected, {socket.assigns.endpoint_order, socket.assigns.extra_domains}, fn key,\n157\t                                                                                              {order,\n158\t                                                                                               extras} ->\n159\t        cond do\n160\t          TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(key) == key ->\n161\t            # A key that IS its own domain (e.g. \"/admin\", \"/health\") is a\n162\t            # domain selection — render it as a domain node, never as a\n163\t            # synthetic satellite.\n164\t            if key in extras or Enum.any?(order, &(TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(&1) == key)) do\n165\t              {order, extras}\n166\t            else\n167\t              {order, extras ++ [key]}\n168\t            end\n169\t\n170\t          key in order ->\n171\t            {order, extras}\n172\t\n173\t          true ->\n174\t            {order ++ [key], extras}\n175\t        end\n176\t      end)\n177\t\n178\t    {:noreply,\n179\t     socket\n180\t     |> assign(:selected, selected)\n181\t     |> assign(:endpoint_order, endpoint_order)\n182\t     |> assign(:extra_domains, extra_domains)}\n183\t  end\n184\t\n185\t  defp normalize_selected(nil), do: []\n186\t  defp normalize_selected(value) when is_binary(value), do: [value]\n187\t\n188\t  defp normalize_selected(values) when is_list(values) do\n189\t    values |> Enum.filter(&is_binary/1) |> Enum.reject(&(&1 == \"\"))\n190\t  end\n191\t\n192\t  defp normalize_selected(_other), do: []\n193\t\n194\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n195\t  # handle_event — node filter toggles (AC5) + level pills (AC9)\n196\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n197\t\n198\t  # An ordinary server round trip (unlike the Live Stream page's\n199\t  # client-side-instant bubbles — no zero-lag constraint applies here, per\n200\t  # the ATDD contract): toggle the key in the selection, then push_patch so\n201\t  # handle_params/3 re-seeds @selected from the URL. The clicked node also\n202\t  # becomes the inspected node (AC8; mirrors the dummy's `activate()`).\n203\t  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n204\t  def handle_event(\"toggle_node\", %{\"key\" => key}, socket) when is_binary(key) do\n205\t    selected = socket.assigns.selected\n206\t\n207\t    new_selected =\n208\t      if key in selected, do: List.delete(selected, key), else: selected ++ [key]\n209\t\n210\t    {:noreply,\n211\t     socket\n212\t     |> assign(:inspected, key)\n213\t     |> push_patch(to: patch_path(new_selected))}\n214\t  end\n215\t\n216\t  def handle_event(\"toggle_node\", _params, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}\n217\t\n218\t  # Clicking nebu (or the stream header's \"Clear all\") clears every filter.\n219\t  def handle_event(\"clear_filters\", _params, socket) do\n220\t    {:noreply, push_patch(socket, to: patch_path([]))}\n221\t  end\n222\t\n223\t  # AC9's leitstand shortcut: all active + click one -> ONLY that one;\n224\t  # the sole active one clicked again -> all active; otherwise plain toggle.\n225\t  def handle_event(\"toggle_level\", %{\"level\" => level}, socket) when level in @ui_levels do\n226\t    active = socket.assigns.active_levels\n227\t    all = MapSet.new(@ui_levels)\n228\t\n229\t    new_active =\n230\t      cond do\n231\t        MapSet.equal?(active, all) -> MapSet.new([level])\n232\t        MapSet.equal?(active, MapSet.new([level])) -> all\n233\t        MapSet.member?(active, level) -> MapSet.delete(active, level)\n234\t        true -> MapSet.put(active, level)\n235\t      end\n236\t\n237\t    {:noreply, assign(socket, :active_levels, new_active)}\n238\t  end\n239\t\n240\t  def handle_event(\"toggle_level\", _params, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}\n241\t\n242\t  # AC5 URL format (story amendment): bracket-array `endpoint[]=...` params,\n243\t  # percent-encoded (`endpoint%5B%5D=...`) — `Plug.Conn.Query.decode/1`\n244\t  # natively decodes this into `%{\"endpoint\" => [...]}` with no data loss.\n245\t  defp patch_path([]), do: @base_path\n246\t\n247\t  defp patch_path(selected) do\n248\t    query = Enum.map_join(selected, \"&\", fn key -> \"endpoint%5B%5D=\" <> URI.encode_www_form(key) end)\n249\t    @base_path <> \"?\" <> query\n250\t  end\n251\t\n252\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n253\t  # handle_info — live PubSub ingest (AC3) + tick (auto-heal) + war-room poll\n254\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n255\t\n256\t  # Advancing `@now_ms` every second is what makes the AC3 window actually\n257\t  # SLIDE for the reader: statuses/counts are pure functions of\n258\t  # `(state, now_ms)` (see TraceMapStatus), so a node with no new traffic\n259\t  # auto-heals to :ok the moment its last warning/error falls out of the\n260\t  # window — no acknowledge, no cleanup pass, just a fresher `now`.\n261\t  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n262\t  def handle_info(:trace_map_tick, socket) do\n263\t    schedule_tick()\n264\t    {:noreply, assign(socket, :now_ms, System.system_time(:millisecond))}\n265\t  end\n266\t\n267\t  # AC6: the banner reacts to sessions starting/stopping while the page is\n268\t  # open. Always rescheduled (unlike the Live Stream page's\n269\t  # only-while-active refresh) because the banner must also APPEAR when a\n270\t  # session starts later.\n271\t  def handle_info(:refresh_war_room_status, socket) do\n272\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo), do: schedule_war_room_refresh()\n273\t    {:noreply, assign(socket, :war_room, initial_war_room_status())}\n274\t  end\n275\t\n276\t  # `Nebu.Web.LogStore.Handler.safe_broadcast/1` broadcasts a BARE MAP (not a\n277\t  # tagged tuple) — same match as `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.handle_info/2`.\n278\t  def handle_info(%{level: _, source: _, request_id: _} = entry, socket) do\n279\t    {:noreply, ingest_entry(socket, entry, announce: true)}\n280\t  end\n281\t\n282\t  def handle_info(_other, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}\n283\t\n284\t  defp schedule_tick, do: Process.send_after(self(), :trace_map_tick, @tick_interval_ms)\n285\t\n286\t  defp schedule_war_room_refresh do\n287\t    Process.send_after(self(), :refresh_war_room_status, @war_room_refresh_interval_ms)\n288\t  end\n289\t\n290\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n291\t  # Ingest\n292\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n293\t\n294\t  defp ingest_entry(socket, entry, opts) do\n295\t    level = normalize_level(entry[:level])\n296\t    route = normalize_route(entry[:route_template])\n297\t    {ts_ms, ts_text} = entry_timestamp(entry)\n298\t\n299\t    node_keys =\n300\t      case route do\n301\t        nil -> [\"nebu\"]\n302\t        route -> [route, TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(route), \"nebu\"]\n303\t      end\n304\t\n305\t    socket\n306\t    |> track_endpoint(route)\n307\t    |> record_event(node_keys, level, ts_ms)\n308\t    |> push_node_event(node_keys, level, ts_text, entry[:message])\n309\t    |> push_stream_line(route, level, ts_text, entry[:message])\n310\t    |> maybe_announce(route, level, ts_ms, opts)\n311\t    |> assign(:now_ms, max(ts_ms, socket.assigns.now_ms))\n312\t  end\n313\t\n314\t  defp track_endpoint(socket, nil), do: socket\n315\t\n316\t  defp track_endpoint(socket, route) do\n317\t    update(socket, :endpoint_order, fn order ->\n318\t      if route in order, do: order, else: order ++ [route]\n319\t    end)\n320\t  end\n321\t\n322\t  # Every entry counts as a :request in its node's window; warnings/errors\n323\t  # are ADDITIONALLY recorded so the badge/tooltip counts match the dummy's\n324\t  # semantics (total = all events, warn/err = level subsets).\n325\t  defp record_event(socket, node_keys, level, ts_ms) do\n326\t    kinds =\n327\t      case level do\n328\t        \"error\" -> [:request, :error]\n329\t        \"warning\" -> [:request, :warning]\n330\t        _ -> [:request]\n331\t      end\n332\t\n333\t    update(socket, :node_states, fn states ->\n334\t      Enum.reduce(node_keys, states, fn key, acc ->\n335\t        state = Map.get(acc, key, TraceMapStatus.new())\n336\t        state = Enum.reduce(kinds, state, &TraceMapStatus.record(&2, &1, ts_ms))\n337\t        Map.put(acc, key, state)\n338\t      end)\n339\t    end)\n340\t  end\n341\t\n342\t  defp push_node_event(socket, node_keys, level, ts_text, message) do\n343\t    event = %{ts: ts_text, level: ui_level(level), message: truncate(message)}\n344\t\n345\t    update(socket, :node_events, fn events ->\n346\t      Enum.reduce(node_keys, events, fn key, acc ->\n347\t        Map.update(acc, key, [event], &Enum.take([event | &1], @max_inspector_events))\n348\t      end)\n349\t    end)\n350\t  end\n351\t\n352\t  defp push_stream_line(socket, route, level, ts_text, message) do\n353\t    line = %{\n354\t      ts: ts_text,\n355\t      ui_level: ui_level(level),\n356\t      route: route,\n357\t      domain: route && TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(route),\n358\t      message: truncate(message)\n359\t    }\n360\t\n361\t    update(socket, :stream_entries, fn lines ->\n362\t      (lines ++ [line]) |> Enum.take(-@max_stream_lines)\n363\t    end)\n364\t  end\n365\t\n366\t  # AC10: aria-live announcements for new errors — throttled (2s debounce),\n367\t  # endpoint name + no message content (spec §8's no-sensitive-data rule).\n368\t  defp maybe_announce(socket, route, \"error\", ts_ms, opts) do\n369\t    if Keyword.get(opts, :announce, true) and is_binary(route) and\n370\t         ts_ms - socket.assigns.last_announce_ms >= @announce_debounce_ms do\n371\t      socket\n372\t      |> assign(:announcement, \"New errors detected in \" <> route)\n373\t      |> assign(:last_announce_ms, ts_ms)\n374\t    else\n375\t      socket\n376\t    end\n377\t  end\n378\t\n379\t  defp maybe_announce(socket, _route, _level, _ts_ms, _opts), do: socket\n380\t\n381\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n382\t  # Data-source guards (DB-free unit-test contract, see moduledoc)\n383\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n384\t\n385\t  defp initial_war_room_status do\n386\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n387\t      Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.status()\n388\t    else\n389\t      :none\n390\t    end\n391\t  end\n392\t\n393\t  defp initial_entries do\n394\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n395\t      {:ok, rows} = Nebu.Web.LogStore.get_logs(%{})\n396\t\n397\t      # most-recent-first from get_logs/1; reverse to oldest-first so the\n398\t      # stream panel reads chronologically and inspector events stack\n399\t      # newest-first correctly.\n400\t      rows |> Enum.take(@history_seed_cap) |> Enum.reverse()\n401\t    else\n402\t      []\n403\t    end\n404\t  end\n405\t\n406\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n407\t  # Normalization helpers\n408\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n409\t\n410\t  defp normalize_level(level) when is_binary(level), do: level\n411\t  defp normalize_level(level) when is_atom(level) and not is_nil(level), do: Atom.to_string(level)\n412\t  defp normalize_level(_), do: \"info\"\n413\t\n414\t  defp normalize_route(nil), do: nil\n415\t  defp normalize_route(\"\"), do: nil\n416\t  defp normalize_route(route) when is_binary(route), do: route\n417\t  defp normalize_route(route), do: to_string(route)\n418\t\n419\t  defp ui_level(\"debug\"), do: \"DEBUG\"\n420\t  defp ui_level(\"warning\"), do: \"WARN\"\n421\t  defp ui_level(\"error\"), do: \"ERROR\"\n422\t  defp ui_level(_), do: \"INFO\"\n423\t\n424\t  defp truncate(nil), do: \"\"\n425\t\n426\t  defp truncate(message) when is_binary(message) do\n427\t    if String.length(message) > 120, do: String.slice(message, 0, 120) <> \"…\", else: message\n428\t  end\n429\t\n430\t  defp truncate(message), do: truncate(to_string(message))\n431\t\n432\t  # Live broadcasts carry no timestamp -> \"now\"; mount-time LogStore history\n433\t  # rows carry `inserted_at` (DateTime/NaiveDateTime) -> converted so the\n434\t  # 60-second window judges seeded rows by their REAL age, not by mount time.\n435\t  defp entry_timestamp(entry) do\n436\t    case entry[:inserted_at] do\n437\t      %DateTime{} = dt ->\n438\t        {DateTime.to_unix(dt, :millisecond), Calendar.strftime(dt, \"%H:%M:%S\")}\n439\t\n440\t      %NaiveDateTime{} = naive ->\n441\t        dt = DateTime.from_naive!(naive, \"Etc/UTC\")\n442\t        {DateTime.to_unix(dt, :millisecond), Calendar.strftime(dt, \"%H:%M:%S\")}\n443\t\n444\t      _ ->\n445\t        now = DateTime.utc_now()\n446\t        {DateTime.to_unix(now, :millisecond), Calendar.strftime(now, \"%H:%M:%S\")}\n447\t    end\n448\t  end\n449\t\n450\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n451\t  # Render\n452\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n453\t\n454\t  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n455\t  def render(assigns) do\n456\t    groups = domain_groups(assigns.endpoint_order, assigns.extra_domains)\n457\t\n458\t    layout =\n459\t      TraceMapGeometry.layout(\n460\t        Enum.map(groups, fn {domain, leaves} -> %{key: domain, satellites: leaves} end)\n461\t      )\n462\t\n463\t    assigns =\n464\t      assigns\n465\t      |> assign(:nodes, build_nodes(groups, layout, assigns))\n466\t      |> assign(:edges, build_edges(groups, layout))\n467\t      |> assign(:visible_lines, visible_lines(assigns))\n468\t      |> assign(:inspected_node, inspected_node(assigns))\n469\t      |> assign(:ui_levels, @ui_levels)\n470\t\n471\t    ~H\"\"\"\n472\t    <div\n473\t      id=\"trace-map-leitstand\"\n474\t      phx-hook=\"TraceMapLeitstand\"\n475\t      class=\"flex flex-col gap-3\"\n476\t      style=\"height: calc(100vh - 6.5rem);\"\n477\t    >\n478\t      <div class=\"flex items-center justify-between shrink-0\">\n479\t        <h1 class=\"text-heading font-semibold\">Trace Map</h1>\n480\t      </div>\n481\t\n482\t      <%!-- AC6: war-room banner — always present in markup, toggled via the\n483\t            boolean `hidden` attribute; role=status/aria-live=polite so an\n484\t            admin starting a session while this page is open gets announced. --%>\n485\t      <%!-- No `flex` utility here, deliberately: an author-stylesheet\n486\t            `.flex { display: flex }` beats the UA's `[hidden] { display: none }`\n487\t            in the cascade, so the \"hidden\" banner would still render (the exact\n488\t            trace-map-hierarchy/admin-sidebar-nesting flex-beats-hidden bug —\n489\t            reconfirmed live in dev cycle 1). `.tm-wr-banner` owns display:flex\n490\t            AND a `[hidden]` override in app.css. --%>\n491\t      <div\n492\t        data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-banner\"\n493\t        role=\"status\"\n494\t        aria-live=\"polite\"\n495\t        hidden={not war_room_active?(@war_room)}\n496\t        class=\"tm-wr-banner items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-error bg-status-error-bg px-4 py-2 text-sm shrink-0\"\n497\t      >\n498\t        <span data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-dot\" class=\"tm-recdot\" aria-hidden=\"true\"></span>\n499\t        <span :if={war_room_active?(@war_room)}>\n500\t          War Room session active — capturing\n501\t          <b class=\"font-mono font-medium\">{war_room_session(@war_room).started_by}</b>\n502\t          · <span\n503\t            data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-elapsed\"\n504\t            id=\"trace-map-war-room-elapsed\"\n505\t            data-started-at={war_room_started_at_unix(@war_room)}\n506\t
