      >{war_room_elapsed(@war_room)}</span>\n507\t          <span :if={war_room_session(@war_room).reason} class=\"text-text-secondary\">\n508\t            · {war_room_session(@war_room).reason}\n509\t          </span>\n510\t        </span>\n511\t        <.link\n512\t          navigate=\"/admin/logs/war-room\"\n513\t          data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-open-link\"\n514\t          class=\"btn btn-outline btn-error btn-xs ml-auto\"\n515\t        >\n516\t          Open War Room\n517\t        </.link>\n518\t      </div>\n519\t\n520\t      <div class=\"flex gap-4 flex-1 min-h-0\">\n521\t        <div class=\"flex-1 flex flex-col min-w-0\" id=\"trace-map-left\">\n522\t          <div class=\"relative flex-1 min-h-0 rounded-lg bg-base-400 border border-base-300\" id=\"trace-map-map-wrap\">\n523\t            <svg\n524\t              data-testid=\"trace-map-svg\"\n525\t              viewBox=\"-560 -400 1120 800\"\n526\t              preserveAspectRatio=\"xMidYMid meet\"\n527\t              role=\"group\"\n528\t              aria-label=\"Service health map\"\n529\t              class=\"w-full h-full block\"\n530\t            >\n531\t              <g>\n532\t                <line\n533\t                  :for={edge <- @edges}\n534\t                  class=\"tm-edge\"\n535\t                  x1={edge.x1}\n536\t                  y1={edge.y1}\n537\t                  x2={edge.x2}\n538\t                  y2={edge.y2}\n539\t                />\n540\t              </g>\n541\t              <g id=\"trace-map-pings\"></g>\n542\t              <g>\n543\t                <.map_node :for={node <- @nodes} node={node} />\n544\t              </g>\n545\t            </svg>\n546\t            <div data-testid=\"trace-map-tooltip\" id=\"trace-map-tooltip\" class=\"tm-tooltip\" hidden></div>\n547\t          </div>\n548\t\n549\t          <%!-- AC9: resizable drag handle — pointer drag + ArrowUp/Down ±32px,\n550\t                Home/End max/min, dblclick resets to 218px (TraceMapLeitstand hook). --%>\n551\t          <div\n552\t            data-testid=\"trace-map-resizer\"\n553\t            id=\"trace-map-resizer\"\n554\t            role=\"separator\"\n555\t            aria-orientation=\"horizontal\"\n556\t            tabindex=\"0\"\n557\t            aria-label=\"Resize log stream (drag, or arrow up/down; double-click resets)\"\n558\t            class=\"tm-resizer shrink-0\"\n559\t          >\n560\t          </div>\n561\t\n562\t          <section\n563\t            data-testid=\"trace-map-stream\"\n564\t            id=\"trace-map-stream\"\n565\t            aria-label=\"Live log stream\"\n566\t            class=\"tm-stream flex flex-col shrink-0 border border-base-300 rounded-b-lg bg-base-100\"\n567\t          >\n568\t            <div class=\"flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap px-3 py-2 border-b border-base-300 shrink-0\">\n569\t              <span class=\"text-caption uppercase text-text-secondary\">Live stream</span>\n570\t              <span role=\"group\" aria-label=\"Level filter\" class=\"inline-flex gap-1\">\n571\t                <button\n572\t                  :for={level <- @ui_levels}\n573\t                  type=\"button\"\n574\t                  data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"\n575\t                  data-level={level}\n576\t                  aria-pressed={to_string(MapSet.member?(@active_levels, level))}\n577\t                  phx-click=\"toggle_level\"\n578\t                  phx-value-level={level}\n579\t                  class={[\n580\t                    \"font-mono text-xs font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border\",\n581\t                    \"focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary\",\n582\t                    level_pill_class(level, MapSet.member?(@active_levels, level))\n583\t                  ]}\n584\t                >\n585\t                  {level}\n586\t                </button>\n587\t              </span>\n588\t              <span\n589\t                :for={key <- @selected}\n590\t                data-testid=\"trace-map-filter-chip\"\n591\t                data-chip-value={key}\n592\t                class=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-mono text-xs border border-primary rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 bg-base-200\"\n593\t              >\n594\t                {key}\n595\t                <button\n596\t                  type=\"button\"\n597\t                  data-testid=\"trace-map-filter-chip-remove\"\n598\t                  phx-click=\"toggle_node\"\n599\t                  phx-value-key={key}\n600\t                  aria-label={\"Remove filter \" <> key}\n601\t                  class=\"text-primary font-bold px-0.5 focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary rounded\"\n602\t                >\n603\t                  ×\n604\t                </button>\n605\t              </span>\n606\t              <button\n607\t                :if={@selected != []}\n608\t                type=\"button\"\n609\t                data-testid=\"trace-map-clear-all\"\n610\t                phx-click=\"clear_filters\"\n611\t                class=\"inline-flex items-center font-mono text-xs border border-base-300 text-text-secondary rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 hover:bg-base-300 focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary\"\n612\t              >\n613\t                Clear all\n614\t              </button>\n615\t            </div>\n616\t            <div id=\"trace-map-stream-body\" class=\"flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-3 py-1 font-mono text-xs\" tabindex=\"0\">\n617\t              <div\n618\t                :for={line <- @visible_lines}\n619\t                data-testid=\"trace-map-stream-line\"\n620\t                data-level={line.ui_level}\n621\t                data-route={line.route || \"\"}\n622\t                class=\"flex gap-2 whitespace-nowrap py-px\"\n623\t              >\n624\t                <span class=\"text-text-disabled\">{line.ts}</span>\n625\t                <span class={[\"w-12 shrink-0 font-bold\", level_text_class(line.ui_level)]}>{line.ui_level}</span>\n626\t                <span class=\"text-base-content\">{line.route}</span>\n627\t                <span class=\"text-text-secondary\">{line.message}</span>\n628\t              </div>\n629\t            </div>\n630\t          </section>\n631\t        </div>\n632\t\n633\t        <%!-- AC8: sticky right column — inspector card + always-visible legend. --%>\n634\t        <aside class=\"w-80 shrink-0 flex flex-col gap-3 overflow-y-auto\">\n635\t          <div data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector\" class=\"rounded-lg border border-base-300 bg-base-200 p-4\">\n636\t            <h2 class=\"text-caption uppercase text-text-secondary font-semibold mb-2\">Node inspector</h2>\n637\t            <p :if={is_nil(@inspected_node)} data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-empty\" class=\"text-sm text-text-disabled\">\n638\t              Hover or select a node to see its live health details.\n639\t            </p>\n640\t            <div :if={@inspected_node}>\n641\t              <div data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-name\" class=\"font-semibold text-base\">\n642\t                {@inspected_node.label}\n643\t              </div>\n644\t              <div data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-route\" class=\"font-mono text-xs text-text-secondary break-all mb-2\">\n645\t                {@inspected_node.route}\n646\t              </div>\n647\t              <span\n648\t                data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-status-pill\"\n649\t                data-status={@inspected_node.status}\n650\t                class={[\n651\t                  \"inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold px-2.5 py-0.5 mb-3\",\n652\t                  status_pill_class(@inspected_node.status)\n653\t                ]}\n654\t              >\n655\t                {status_pill_text(@inspected_node.status)}\n656\t              </span>\n657\t              <div class=\"flex justify-between text-sm text-text-secondary py-0.5\">\n658\t                <span>Requests / 60 s</span>\n659\t                <b data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-stat-requests\" class=\"font-mono font-medium text-base-content\">\n660\t                  {@inspected_node.counts.requests}\n661\t                </b>\n662\t              </div>\n663\t              <div class=\"flex justify-between text-sm text-text-secondary py-0.5\">\n664\t                <span>Warnings / 60 s</span>\n665\t                <b data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-stat-warnings\" class=\"font-mono font-medium text-base-content\">\n666\t                  {@inspected_node.counts.warnings}\n667\t                </b>\n668\t              </div>\n669\t              <div class=\"flex justify-between text-sm text-text-secondary py-0.5\">\n670\t                <span>Errors / 60 s</span>\n671\t                <b data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-stat-errors\" class=\"font-mono font-medium text-base-content\">\n672\t                  {@inspected_node.counts.errors}\n673\t                </b>\n674\t              </div>\n675\t              <h3 class=\"text-caption uppercase text-text-secondary font-semibold mt-3 mb-1\">Recent events</h3>\n676\t              <p :if={@inspected_node.events == []} class=\"font-mono text-xs text-text-disabled\">—</p>\n677\t              <div\n678\t                :for={event <- @inspected_node.events}\n679\t                data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-event\"\n680\t                class=\"font-mono text-xs text-text-secondary py-px\"\n681\t              >\n682\t                {event.ts} · {event.level} · {event.message}\n683\t              </div>\n684\t            </div>\n685\t          </div>\n686\t\n687\t          <div data-testid=\"trace-map-legend\" class=\"rounded-lg border border-base-300 bg-base-200 p-4\">\n688\t            <h2 class=\"text-caption uppercase text-text-secondary font-semibold mb-2\">Legend</h2>\n689\t            <div\n690\t              :for={{state, dot_class, text} <- legend_rows()}\n691\t              data-testid=\"trace-map-legend-row\"\n692\t              data-legend-state={state}\n693\t              class=\"flex items-center gap-2.5 py-1 text-sm text-text-secondary\"\n694\t            >\n695\t              <span class={[\"tm-legend-dot\", dot_class]} aria-hidden=\"true\"></span>\n696\t              <span>{text}</span>\n697\t            </div>\n698\t            <p class=\"text-xs text-text-disabled mt-2\">\n699\t              Domains inherit the worst status of their satellites; nebu inherits the worst domain.\n700\t              Nodes heal automatically once the 60 s window passes.\n701\t            </p>\n702\t          </div>\n703\t        </aside>\n704\t      </div>\n705\t\n706\t      <%!-- AC10: throttled aria-live announcer for new errors (endpoint name\n707\t            + no message content — spec §8). --%>\n708\t      <div data-testid=\"trace-map-announcer\" role=\"status\" aria-live=\"polite\" class=\"sr-only\">\n709\t        {@announcement}\n710\t      </div>\n711\t    </div>\n712\t    \"\"\"\n713\t  end\n714\t\n715\t  # ── One SVG map node (nebu / domain / satellite) ──────────────────────────\n716\t  #\n717\t  # Focusable `role=\"button\"` per AC10; Enter/Space activation is wired by\n718\t  # the TraceMapLeitstand hook (SVG <g> has no native keyboard activation).\n719\t  # `aria-pressed` reflects selection; nebu is never a toggle target (always\n720\t  # \"false\" — clicking it CLEARS filters instead).\n721\t  attr(:node, :map, required: true)\n722\t\n723\t  defp map_node(assigns) do\n724\t    ~H\"\"\"\n725\t    <g\n726\t      id={@node.dom_id}\n727\t      data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"\n728\t      data-node-key={@node.key}\n729\t      data-node-kind={@node.kind}\n730\t      data-node-status={@node.status}\n731\t      data-node-selected={to_string(@node.selected)}\n732\t      role=\"button\"\n733\t      tabindex=\"0\"\n734\t      aria-pressed={to_string(@node.pressed)}\n735\t      aria-label={@node.aria_label}\n736\t      data-tooltip-name={@node.label}\n737\t      data-tooltip-route={@node.route}\n738\t      data-tooltip-status={@node.tooltip_status}\n739\t      data-tooltip-counts={@node.tooltip_counts}\n740\t      data-tooltip-cta={@node.tooltip_cta}\n741\t      transform={\"translate(#{@node.x} #{@node.y})\"}\n742\t      phx-click={@node.click_event}\n743\t      phx-value-key={@node.kind != \"nebu\" && @node.key}\n744\t      class={\"tm-node tm-node--\" <> @node.status}\n745\t    >\n746\t      <circle class=\"tm-selring\" r={@node.r + 7} />\n747\t      <circle data-testid=\"trace-map-node-core\" class=\"tm-node-core\" r={@node.r} />\n748\t      <%= case @node.kind do %>\n749\t        <% \"nebu\" -> %>\n750\t          <text class=\"tm-nname\" y=\"-2\">nebu</text>\n751\t          <text class=\"tm-ncount\" y=\"16\">{@node.counts.requests}/min</text>\n752\t        <% \"domain\" -> %>\n753\t          <text class=\"tm-nname\" y=\"-1\">{@node.label}</text>\n754\t          <text class=\"tm-ncount\" y=\"14\">{@node.counts.requests}</text>\n755\t        <% _satellite -> %>\n756\t          <text class=\"tm-ncount\" y=\"4\">{@node.counts.requests}</text>\n757\t          <text class=\"tm-nlabel\" y={@node.r + 15}>{@node.label}</text>\n758\t      <% end %>\n759\t      <g\n760\t        :if={@node.status == \"warn\"}\n761\t        data-testid=\"trace-map-badge-warn\"\n762\t        class=\"tm-badge tm-badge--warn\"\n763\t        transform={badge_transform(@node.r)}\n764\t      >\n765\t        <circle r=\"9\" />\n766\t        <text y=\"3.5\">▲{badge_count(@node.counts.warnings)}</text>\n767\t      </g>\n768\t      <g\n769\t        :if={@node.status == \"error\"}\n770\t        data-testid=\"trace-map-badge-error\"\n771\t        class=\"tm-badge tm-badge--error\"\n772\t        transform={badge_transform(@node.r)}\n773\t      >\n774\t        <circle r=\"9\" />\n775\t        <text y=\"3.5\">✕{badge_count(@node.counts.errors)}</text>\n776\t      </g>\n777\t    </g>\n778\t    \"\"\"\n779\t  end\n780\t\n781\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n782\t  # View-model builders\n783\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n784\t\n785\t  # Groups observed endpoints by domain (REUSING TraceMapTree's canonical\n786\t  # heuristic), then appends URL-selected domains no traffic has produced a\n787\t  # satellite for yet.\n788\t  defp domain_groups(endpoint_order, extra_domains) do\n789\t    groups = TraceMapTree.group_endpoints_by_domain(endpoint_order)\n790\t    known = Enum.map(groups, fn {domain, _leaves} -> domain end)\n791\t\n792\t    groups ++ (extra_domains |> Enum.reject(&(&1 in known)) |> Enum.map(&{&1, []}))\n793\t  end\n794\t\n795\t  # DOM order (AC10 natural Tab order): nebu first, then each domain followed\n796\t  # by its own satellites.\n797\t  defp build_nodes(groups, layout, assigns) do\n798\t    now = assigns.now_ms\n799\t    states = assigns.node_states\n800\t    selected = assigns.selected\n801\t\n802\t    satellite_statuses =\n803\t      Map.new(layout.satellites, fn {key, _pos} ->\n804\t        {key, TraceMapStatus.status(node_state(states, key), now)}\n805\t      end)\n806\t\n807\t    domain_statuses =\n808\t      Map.new(groups, fn {domain, leaves} ->\n809\t        child_statuses = Enum.map(leaves, &Map.fetch!(satellite_statuses, &1))\n810\t        own = TraceMapStatus.status(node_state(states, domain), now)\n811\t        {domain, TraceMapStatus.worst([own | child_statuses])}\n812\t      end)\n813\t\n814\t    nebu_status =\n815\t      TraceMapStatus.worst([\n816\t        TraceMapStatus.status(node_state(states, \"nebu\"), now)\n817\t        | Map.values(domain_statuses)\n818\t      ])\n819\t\n820\t    nebu_node =\n821\t      build_node(\"nebu\", \"nebu\", layout.nebu, nebu_status, \"nebu\", \"all traffic\", selected, states, now)\n822\t\n823\t    domain_and_satellite_nodes =\n824\t      Enum.flat_map(groups, fn {domain, leaves} ->\n825\t        domain_node =\n826\t          build_node(\n827\t            domain,\n828\t            \"domain\",\n829\t            Map.fetch!(layout.domains, domain),\n830\t            Map.fetch!(domain_statuses, domain),\n831\t            domain_label(domain),\n832\t            domain,\n833\t            selected,\n834\t            states,\n835\t            now\n836\t          )\n837\t\n838\t        satellite_nodes =\n839\t          Enum.map(leaves, fn route ->\n840\t            build_node(\n841\t              route,\n842\t              \"satellite\",\n843\t              Map.fetch!(layout.satellites, route),\n844\t              Map.fetch!(satellite_statuses, route),\n845\t              satellite_label(route),\n846\t              route,\n847\t              selected,\n848\t              states,\n849\t              now\n850\t            )\n851\t          end)\n852\t\n853\t        [domain_node | satellite_nodes]\n854\t      end)\n855\t\n856\t    [nebu_node | domain_and_satellite_nodes]\n857\t  end\n858\t\n859\t  defp build_node(key, kind, position, status, label, route, selected, states, now) do\n860\t    counts = TraceMapStatus.counts(node_state(states, key), now)\n861\t    status_string = Atom.to_string(status)\n862\t    is_selected = kind != \"nebu\" and key in selected\n863\t\n864\t    %{\n865\t      key: key,\n866\t      kind: kind,\n867\t      # A stable DOM id per node: LiveView's DOM patching matches keyed\n868\t      # elements in place, so a selection/status patch can never replace the\n869\t      # <g> element itself — keyboard focus survives Enter/Space toggles\n870\t      # (AC10; verified against the live stack in dev cycle 1).\n871\t      dom_id: \"tm-node-\" <> Base.url_encode64(key, padding: false),\n872\t      x: coord(position.x),\n873\t      y: coord(position.y),\n874\t      r: position.r,\n875\t      status: status_string,\n876\t      selected: is_selected,\n877\t      pressed: is_selected,\n878\t      label: label,\n879\t      route: route,\n880\t      counts: counts,\n881\t      aria_label: node_aria_label(kind, label, route, status, counts),\n882\t      tooltip_status: status_word(status),\n883\t      tooltip_counts: counts_text(counts),\n884\t      tooltip_cta: tooltip_cta(kind),\n885\t      click_event: if(kind == \"nebu\", do: \"clear_filters\", else: \"toggle_node\")\n886\t    }\n887\t  end\n888\t\n889\t  defp node_state(states, key), do: Map.get(states, key, TraceMapStatus.new())\n890\t\n891\t  defp build_edges(groups, layout) do\n892\t    domain_edges =\n893\t      Enum.map(layout.domains, fn {_key, domain} ->\n894\t        edge(layout.nebu, domain)\n895\t      end)\n896\t\n897\t    satellite_edges =\n898\t      Enum.flat_map(groups, fn {domain, leaves} ->\n899\t        domain_pos = Map.fetch!(layout.domains, domain)\n900\t        Enum.map(leaves, fn route -> edge(domain_pos, Map.fetch!(layout.satellites, route)) end)\n901\t      end)\n902\t\n903\t    domain_edges ++ satellite_edges\n904\t  end\n905\t\n906\t  defp edge(a, b) do\n907\t    {x1, y1, x2, y2} = TraceMapGeometry.trim_edge(a, b)\n908\t    %{x1: coord(x1), y1: coord(y1), x2: coord(x2), y2: coord(y2)}\n909\t  end\n910\t\n911\t  defp coord(value), do: :erlang.float_to_binary(value / 1, decimals: 2)\n912\t\n913\t  defp visible_lines(assigns) do\n914\t    Enum.filter(assigns.stream_entries, fn line ->\n915\t      MapSet.member?(assigns.active_levels, line.ui_level) and\n916\t        endpoint_match?(line, assigns.selected)\n917\t    end)\n918\t  end\n919\t\n920\t  # AC5: OR within the endpoint dimension; a selected DOMAIN matches all its\n921\t  # satellites' lines.\n922\t  defp endpoint_match?(_line, []), do: true\n923\t\n924\t  defp endpoint_match?(line, selected) do\n925\t    line.route in selected or (line.domain != nil and line.domain in selected)\n926\t  end\n927\t\n928\t  defp inspected_node(%{inspected: nil}), do: nil\n929\t\n930\t  defp inspected_node(assigns) do\n931\t    key = assigns.inspected\n932\t    now = assigns.now_ms\n933\t    state = node_state(assigns.node_states, key)\n934\t\n935\t    {label, route} =\n936\t      cond do\n937\t        key == \"nebu\" -> {\"nebu\", \"all traffic\"}\n938\t        TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(key) == key -> {domain_label(key), key}\n939\t        true -> {satellite_label(key), key}\n940\t      end\n941\t\n942\t    %{\n943\t      label: label,\n944\t      route: route,\n945\t      status: state |> TraceMapStatus.status(now) |> Atom.to_string(),\n946\t      counts: TraceMapStatus.counts(state, now),\n947\t      events: Map.get(assigns.node_events, key, [])\n948\t    }\n949\t  end\n950\t\n951\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n952\t  # Labels / text helpers\n953\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n954\t\n955\t  defp domain_label(domain), do: Map.get(@domain_labels, domain, domain)\n956\t\n957\t  defp satellite_label(route) do\n958\t    route\n959\t    |> String.split(\"/\")\n960\t    |> Enum.reject(&(&1 == \"\"))\n961\t    |> List.last()\n962\t    |> case do\n963\t      nil -> route\n964\t      segment -> segment\n965\t    end\n966\t  end\n967\t\n968\t  # AC10: name + route + status + counts, e.g.\n969\t  # \"users endpoint · /admin/users · errors · 1 error · 0 warnings · 1 request in the last 60 seconds\"\n970\t  defp node_aria_label(kind, label, route, status, counts) do\n971\t    name =\n972\t      case kind do\n973\t        \"nebu\" -> \"nebu\"\n974\t        \"domain\" -> label <> \" domain\"\n975\t        _ -> label <> \" endpoint\"\n976\t      end\n977\t\n978\t    Enum.join(\n979\t      [\n980\t        name,\n981\t        route,\n982\t        status_word(status),\n983\t        pluralize(counts.errors, \"error\"),\n984\t        pluralize(counts.warnings, \"warning\"),\n985\t        pluralize(counts.requests, \"request\") <> \" in the last 60 seconds\"\n986\t      ],\n987\t      \" · \"\n988\t    )\n989\t  end\n990\t\n991\t  defp status_word(:ok), do: \"healthy\"\n992\t  defp status_word(:warn), do: \"warnings\"\n993\t  defp status_word(:error), do: \"errors\"\n994\t\n995\t  defp counts_text(counts) do\n996\t    \"#{pluralize(counts.requests, \"request\")} · #{pluralize(counts.warnings, \"warning\")} · \" <>\n997\t      \"#{pluralize(counts.errors, \"error\")} in the last 60s\"\n998\t  end\n999\t\n1000\t  defp pluralize(1, word), do: \"1 \" <> word\n1001\t  defp pluralize(n, word), do: \"#{n} #{word}s\"\n1002\t\n1003\t  defp tooltip_cta(\"nebu\"), do: \"Click to clear all filters\"\n1004\t  defp tooltip_cta(_kind), do: \"Click to filter the stream\"\n1005\t\n1006\t  defp badge_count(count) when count > 9, do: \"9+\"\n1007\t  defp badge_count(count), do: Integer.to_string(count)\n1008\t\n1009\t  defp badge_transform(radius) do\n1010\t    offset = coord(radius * 0.72)\n1011\t    \"translate(#{offset} -#{offset})\"\n1012\t  end\n1013\t\n1014\t  defp legend_rows do\n1015\t    [\n1016\t      {\"ok\", \"tm-legend-dot--ok\", \"Healthy — recedes into the background\"},\n1017\t      {\"warn\", \"tm-legend-dot--warn\", \"▲ Warnings in the last 60 s\"},\n1018\t      {\"error\", \"tm-legend-dot--error\", \"✕ Errors in the last 60 s — pulses\"},\n1019\t      {\"selected\", \"tm-legend-dot--selected\", \"Selected as stream filter\"},\n1020\t      {\"off\", \"tm-legend-dot--off\",\n1021\t       \"Long-polling endpoint — logging off by default; click to capture for 5 min\"},\n1022\t      {\"temp\", \"tm-legend-dot--temp\", \"Dashed = temporary capture, falls back to off automatically\"}\n1023\t    ]\n1024\t  end\n1025\t\n1026\t  defp level_pill_class(\"DEBUG\", true), do: \"text-text-secondary border-text-secondary\"\n1027\t  defp level_pill_class(\"INFO\", true), do: \"text-base-content border-text-secondary\"\n1028\t  defp level_pill_class(\"WARN\", true), do: \"text-warning border-warning bg-status-warn-bg\"\n1029\t  defp level_pill_class(\"ERROR\", true), do: \"text-error border-error bg-status-error-bg\"\n1030\t  defp level_pill_class(_level, false), do: \"text-text-disabled border-base-300\"\n1031\t\n1032\t  defp level_text_class(\"ERROR\"), do: \"text-error\"\n1033\t  defp level_text_class(\"WARN\"), do: \"text-warning\"\n1034\t  defp level_text_class(\"DEBUG\"), do: \"text-text-disabled\"\n1035\t  defp level_text_class(_), do: \"text-text-secondary\"\n1036\t\n1037\t  defp status_pill_class(\"ok\"), do: \"bg-status-ok-bg text-success\"\n1038\t  defp status_pill_class(\"warn\"), do: \"bg-status-warn-bg text-warning\"\n1039\t  defp status_pill_class(\"error\"), do: \"bg-status-error-bg text-error\"\n1040\t\n1041\t  defp status_pill_text(\"ok\"), do: \"● Healthy\"\n1042\t  defp status_pill_text(\"warn\"), do: \"▲ Warnings\"\n1043\t  defp status_pill_text(\"error\"), do: \"✕ Errors\"\n1044\t\n1045\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n1046\t  # War-room banner helpers (AC6)\n1047\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n1048\t\n1049\t  defp war_room_active?({:ok, _session}), do: true\n1050\t  defp war_room_active?(_), do: false\n1051\t\n1052\t  # Only ever called inside `:if={war_room_active?(@war_room)}` blocks, so a\n1053\t  # fallback clause would be dead code (flagged by --warnings-as-errors).\n1054\t  defp war_room_session({:ok, session}), do: session\n1055\t\n1056\t  defp war_room_started_at_unix({:ok, %{started_at: %DateTime{} = started_at}}) do\n1057\t    Integer.to_string(DateTime.to_unix(started_at))\n1058\t  end\n1059\t\n1060\t  defp war_room_started_at_unix(_), do: nil\n1061\t\n1062\t  defp war_room_elapsed({:ok, %{started_at: %DateTime{} = started_at}}) do\n1063\t    total = max(DateTime.diff(DateTime.utc_now(), started_at, :second), 0)\n1064\t    format_clock(total)\n1065\t  end\n1066\t\n1067\t  defp war_room_elapsed(_), do: \"00:00\"\n1068\t\n1069\t  defp format_clock(total_seconds) when total_seconds >= 3600 do\n1070\t    hours = div(total_seconds, 3600)\n1071\t    rest = rem(total_seconds, 3600)\n1072\t    \"#{pad2(hours)}:#{pad2(div(rest, 60))}:#{pad2(rem(rest, 60))}\"\n1073\t  end\n1074\t\n1075\t  defp format_clock(total_seconds) do\n1076\t    \"#{pad2(div(total_seconds, 60))}:#{pad2(rem(total_seconds, 60))}\"\n1077\t  end\n1078\t\n1079\t  defp pad2(n), do: String.pad_leading(Integer.to_string(n), 2, \"0\")\n1080\tend\n1081\t"}]},"uuid":"1056baa6-b373-402b-bd35-4d0a24a96c4f","timestamp":"2026-07-07T17:12:31.444Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map.ex","content":"defmodule Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  Trace Map \"Leitstand\" — the dedicated, full-page radial health map at\n  `/admin/logs/trace-map` (enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand).\n\n  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand.md\n  Design: _bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-spec.md\n  Visual reference: trace-map-leitstand-dummy.html (same directory).\n\n  ## Why this is a SEPARATE LiveView module, not a new action on `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`\n\n  AC5 hard-requires URL-driven filter state via `push_patch/2` +\n  `handle_params/3` (the lesson of trace-map-filter-reset-bugfix: filter\n  state must live in LiveView assigns seeded from the URL, NEVER in\n  client-DOM-only state). Merely EXPORTING a `handle_params/3` callback on a\n  LiveView module makes every `live_isolated/3`-mounted test of that module\n  fail at mount (empirically verified against the installed\n  `phoenix_live_view` — see `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s own moduledoc). The\n  existing `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive` (Live Stream page) has a large,\n  deliberately-`live_isolated/3`-based suite, so the Leitstand gets its own\n  module and `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive` stays `handle_params/3`-free.\n\n  ## State model\n\n    * `@selected` — the active endpoint filters (satellite routes and/or\n      domain keys), URL-driven: `handle_params/3` seeds it from the\n      bracket-array `?endpoint[]=X&endpoint[]=Y` query params; every node\n      click round-trips through `handle_event(\"toggle_node\"/\"clear_filters\")`\n      -> `push_patch/2` -> `handle_params/3`. Bracket-array (not the story's\n      literal repeated bare `?endpoint=X&endpoint=Y`) per the story's own\n      \"Story Amendments\": `Plug.Conn.Query.decode/1` is last-wins on repeated\n      bare keys, so the bare form cannot round-trip.\n    * `@node_states` — one `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapStatus` state per map\n      node (`\"nebu\"`, each domain key, each satellite route): the AC3\n      60-second sliding window. Events are recorded at ALL THREE levels on\n      ingest so every node carries its own windowed request/warning/error\n      counts; a parent's STATUS is still derived via\n      `TraceMapStatus.worst/1` over its children (worst-child propagation,\n      spec §2), with the parent's own aggregated state included defensively\n      (covers nil-route entries that belong to no domain, for nebu).\n    * `@active_levels` — the stream panel's level filter (AC9), plain\n      assigns state (the leitstand shortcut is computed server-side in\n      `handle_event(\"toggle_level\", ...)`).\n    * Geometry is computed server-side by `Nebu.Admin.TraceMapGeometry`\n      (spec §8 constraint 3); domain grouping REUSES\n      `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint/1` via\n      `group_endpoints_by_domain/1` (never duplicated).\n\n  ## PubSub / data source\n\n  Same source as the Live Stream page: mount-time history from\n  `Nebu.Web.LogStore.get_logs/1` (guarded behind `Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo)`\n  so plain unit-test runs stay DB-free) + live `\"operational_logs\"` PubSub\n  broadcasts of bare entry maps.\n\n  ## No sensitive data (spec §8 constraint 5)\n\n  The map, tooltips, aria-labels and the live announcer only ever carry\n  route templates + counts. Log MESSAGE text appears only in the stream\n  panel and the inspector's recent-events list — the same already-redacted\n  `message` field the Live Stream page shows (LogStore's writer owns\n  redaction) — never in tooltips/labels/announcements.\n\n  ## CSP (spec §8 constraint 2)\n\n  No inline JS anywhere. Client-only behavior (tooltip positioning, resizer\n  drag/keyboard, Enter/Space activation of SVG nodes, stream autoscroll,\n  war-room elapsed clock, status-transition pings) lives in the\n  `TraceMapLeitstand` phx-hook in `server/priv/static/assets/app.js`;\n  animations (error pulse, recording dot, pings) are pure CSS `@keyframes`\n  in `server/assets/css/app.css`, disabled under `prefers-reduced-motion`.\n  \"\"\"\n  use Phoenix.LiveView, layout: {Nebu.Admin.Layouts, :live}\n\n  alias Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapStatus\n  alias Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapTree\n  alias Nebu.Admin.TraceMapGeometry\n\n  @topic \"operational_logs\"\n  @base_path \"/admin/logs/trace-map\"\n\n  # UI level-pill vocabulary (spec §7) <-> LogStore level strings. The pill\n  # labels are the spec's abbreviated forms; `Nebu.Web.LogStore` entries use\n  # \"warning\"/\"error\" — mapped via ui_level/1 below.\n  @ui_levels [\"DEBUG\", \"INFO\", \"WARN\", \"ERROR\"]\n\n  @domain_labels %{\n    \"/_matrix/client\" => \"Matrix Client\",\n    \"/_matrix/media\" => \"Matrix Media\",\n    \"/admin\" => \"Admin\",\n    \"/health\" => \"Health\"\n  }\n\n  # Bounded in-memory buffers (per-LiveView-process).\n  @max_stream_lines 250\n  @max_inspector_events 5\n  @history_seed_cap 200\n\n  @tick_interval_ms 1_000\n  @war_room_refresh_interval_ms 2_000\n  @announce_debounce_ms 2_000\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Mount / handle_params\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n  def mount(_params, _session, socket) do\n    Phoenix.PubSub.subscribe(Nebu.PubSub, @topic)\n\n    socket =\n      socket\n      # admin-sidebar-nesting coordination (AC1 / UX gate advisory A2): the\n      # Leitstand highlights the \"Trace Map\" CHILD of the nested Logs section.\n      |> assign(:active_nav, \"logs-trace-map\")\n      |> assign(:page_title, \"Trace Map\")\n      |> assign(:endpoint_order, [])\n      |> assign(:extra_domains, [])\n      |> assign(:node_states, %{})\n      |> assign(:node_events, %{})\n      |> assign(:stream_entries, [])\n      |> assign(:selected, [])\n      |> assign(:active_levels, MapSet.new(@ui_levels))\n      |> assign(:inspected, nil)\n      |> assign(:war_room, initial_war_room_status())\n      |> assign(:announcement, \"\")\n      |> assign(:last_announce_ms, 0)\n      |> assign(:now_ms, System.system_time(:millisecond))\n\n    socket =\n      Enum.reduce(initial_entries(), socket, fn entry, acc ->\n        ingest_entry(acc, entry, announce: false)\n      end)\n\n    if connected?(socket) do\n      schedule_tick()\n      # `WarRoomManager.status/0` is a live DB read — only poll when\n      # `Nebu.Repo` is running (mirrors `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s guard).\n      if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo), do: schedule_war_room_refresh()\n    end\n\n    {:ok, socket}\n  end\n\n  # AC5: URL query params are the single source of truth for the endpoint\n  # selection — seeded here on mount AND after every push_patch round trip.\n  # A bookmarked URL may select endpoints/domains no traffic has been\n  # observed for yet: those are added to the map (as satellite or\n  # domain-only nodes) so the restored view renders its selection rings and\n  # chips immediately (bookmarkable-URL contract, AC5/AC12 Scenario 10).\n  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n  def handle_params(params, _uri, socket) do\n    selected = params |> Map.get(\"endpoint\") |> normalize_selected()\n\n    {endpoint_order, extra_domains} =\n      Enum.reduce(selected, {socket.assigns.endpoint_order, socket.assigns.extra_domains}, fn key,\n                                                                                              {order,\n                                                                                               extras} ->\n        cond do\n          TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(key) == key ->\n            # A key that IS its own domain (e.g. \"/admin\", \"/health\") is a\n            # domain selection — render it as a domain node, never as a\n            # synthetic satellite.\n            if key in extras or Enum.any?(order, &(TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(&1) == key)) do\n              {order, extras}\n            else\n              {order, extras ++ [key]}\n            end\n\n          key in order ->\n            {order, extras}\n\n          true ->\n            {order ++ [key], extras}\n        end\n      end)\n\n    {:noreply,\n     socket\n     |> assign(:selected, selected)\n     |> assign(:endpoint_order, endpoint_order)\n     |> assign(:extra_domains, extra_domains)}\n  end\n\n  defp normalize_selected(nil), do: []\n  defp normalize_selected(value) when is_binary(value), do: [value]\n\n  defp normalize_selected(values) when is_list(values) do\n    values |> Enum.filter(&is_binary/1) |> Enum.reject(&(&1 == \"\"))\n  end\n\n  defp normalize_selected(_other), do: []\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # handle_event — node filter toggles (AC5) + level pills (AC9)\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  # An ordinary server round trip (unlike the Live Stream page's\n  # client-side-instant bubbles — no zero-lag constraint applies here, per\n  # the ATDD contract): toggle the key in the selection, then push_patch so\n  # handle_params/3 re-seeds @selected from the URL. The clicked node also\n  # becomes the inspected node (AC8; mirrors the dummy's `activate()`).\n  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n  def handle_event(\"toggle_node\", %{\"key\" => key}, socket) when is_binary(key) do\n    selected = socket.assigns.selected\n\n    new_selected =\n      if key in selected, do: List.delete(selected, key), else: selected ++ [key]\n\n    {:noreply,\n     socket\n     |> assign(:inspected, key)\n     |> push_patch(to: patch_path(new_selected))}\n  end\n\n  def handle_event(\"toggle_node\", _params, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}\n\n  # Clicking nebu (or the stream header's \"Clear all\") clears every filter.\n  def handle_event(\"clear_filters\", _params, socket) do\n    {:noreply, push_patch(socket, to: patch_path([]))}\n  end\n\n  # AC9's leitstand shortcut: all active + click one -> ONLY that one;\n  # the sole active one clicked again -> all active; otherwise plain toggle.\n  def handle_event(\"toggle_level\", %{\"level\" => level}, socket) when level in @ui_levels do\n    active = socket.assigns.active_levels\n    all = MapSet.new(@ui_levels)\n\n    new_active =\n      cond do\n        MapSet.equal?(active, all) -> MapSet.new([level])\n        MapSet.equal?(active, MapSet.new([level])) -> all\n        MapSet.member?(active, level) -> MapSet.delete(active, level)\n        true -> MapSet.put(active, level)\n      end\n\n    {:noreply, assign(socket, :active_levels, new_active)}\n  end\n\n  def handle_event(\"toggle_level\", _params, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}\n\n  # AC5 URL format (story amendment): bracket-array `endpoint[]=...` params,\n  # percent-encoded (`endpoint%5B%5D=...`) — `Plug.Conn.Query.decode/1`\n  # natively decodes this into `%{\"endpoint\" => [...]}` with no data loss.\n  defp patch_path([]), do: @base_path\n\n  defp patch_path(selected) do\n    query = Enum.map_join(selected, \"&\", fn key -> \"endpoint%5B%5D=\" <> URI.encode_www_form(key) end)\n    @base_path <> \"?\" <> query\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # handle_info — live PubSub ingest (AC3) + tick (auto-heal) + war-room poll\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  # Advancing `@now_ms` every second is what makes the AC3 window actually\n  # SLIDE for the reader: statuses/counts are pure functions of\n  # `(state, now_ms)` (see TraceMapStatus), so a node with no new traffic\n  # auto-heals to :ok the moment its last warning/error falls out of the\n  # window — no acknowledge, no cleanup pass, just a fresher `now`.\n  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n  def handle_info(:trace_map_tick, socket) do\n    schedule_tick()\n    {:noreply, assign(socket, :now_ms, System.system_time(:millisecond))}\n  end\n\n  # AC6: the banner reacts to sessions starting/stopping while the page is\n  # open. Always rescheduled (unlike the Live Stream page's\n  # only-while-active refresh) because the banner must also APPEAR when a\n  # session starts later.\n  def handle_info(:refresh_war_room_status, socket) do\n    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo), do: schedule_war_room_refresh()\n    {:noreply, assign(socket, :war_room, initial_war_room_status())}\n  end\n\n  # `Nebu.Web.LogStore.Handler.safe_broadcast/1` broadcasts a BARE MAP (not a\n  # tagged tuple) — same match as `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.handle_info/2`.\n  def handle_info(%{level: _, source: _, request_id: _} = entry, socket) do\n    {:noreply, ingest_entry(socket, entry, announce: true)}\n  end\n\n  def handle_info(_other, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}\n\n  defp schedule_tick, do: Process.send_after(self(), :trace_map_tick, @tick_interval_ms)\n\n  defp schedule_war_room_refresh do\n    Process.send_after(self(), :refresh_war_room_status, @war_room_refresh_interval_ms)\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Ingest\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  defp ingest_entry(socket, entry, opts) do\n    level = normalize_level(entry[:level])\n    route = normalize_route(entry[:route_template])\n    {ts_ms, ts_text} = entry_timestamp(entry)\n\n    node_keys =\n      case route do\n        nil -> [\"nebu\"]\n        route -> [route, TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(route), \"nebu\"]\n      end\n\n    socket\n    |> track_endpoint(route)\n    |> record_event(node_keys, level, ts_ms)\n    |> push_node_event(node_keys, level, ts_text, entry[:message])\n    |> push_stream_line(route, level, ts_text, entry[:message])\n    |> maybe_announce(route, level, ts_ms, opts)\n    |> assign(:now_ms, max(ts_ms, socket.assigns.now_ms))\n  end\n\n  defp track_endpoint(socket, nil), do: socket\n\n  defp track_endpoint(socket, route) do\n    update(socket, :endpoint_order, fn order ->\n      if route in order, do: order, else: order ++ [route]\n    end)\n  end\n\n  # Every entry counts as a :request in its node's window; warnings/errors\n  # are ADDITIONALLY recorded so the badge/tooltip counts match the dummy's\n  # semantics (total = all events, warn/err = level subsets).\n  defp record_event(socket, node_keys, level, ts_ms) do\n    kinds =\n      case level do\n        \"error\" -> [:request, :error]\n        \"warning\" -> [:request, :warning]\n        _ -> [:request]\n      end\n\n    update(socket, :node_states, fn states ->\n      Enum.reduce(node_keys, states, fn key, acc ->\n        state = Map.get(acc, key, TraceMapStatus.new())\n        state = Enum.reduce(kinds, state, &TraceMapStatus.record(&2, &1, ts_ms))\n        Map.put(acc, key, state)\n      end)\n    end)\n  end\n\n  defp push_node_event(socket, node_keys, level, ts_text, message) do\n    event = %{ts: ts_text, level: ui_level(level), message: truncate(message)}\n\n    update(socket, :node_events, fn events ->\n      Enum.reduce(node_keys, events, fn key, acc ->\n        Map.update(acc, key, [event], &Enum.take([event | &1], @max_inspector_events))\n      end)\n    end)\n  end\n\n  defp push_stream_line(socket, route, level, ts_text, message) do\n    line = %{\n      ts: ts_text,\n      ui_level: ui_level(level),\n      route: route,\n      domain: route && TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(route),\n      message: truncate(message)\n    }\n\n    update(socket, :stream_entries, fn lines ->\n      (lines ++ [line]) |> Enum.take(-@max_stream_lines)\n    end)\n  end\n\n  # AC10: aria-live announcements for new errors — throttled (2s debounce),\n  # endpoint name + no message content (spec §8's no-sensitive-data rule).\n  defp maybe_announce(socket, route, \"error\", ts_ms, opts) do\n    if Keyword.get(opts, :announce, true) and is_binary(route) and\n         ts_ms - socket.assigns.last_announce_ms >= @announce_debounce_ms do\n      socket\n      |> assign(:announcement, \"New errors detected in \" <> route)\n      |> assign(:last_announce_ms, ts_ms)\n    else\n      socket\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp maybe_announce(socket, _route, _level, _ts_ms, _opts), do: socket\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Data-source guards (DB-free unit-test contract, see moduledoc)\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  defp initial_war_room_status do\n    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n      Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.status()\n    else\n      :none\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp initial_entries do\n    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n      {:ok, rows} = Nebu.Web.LogStore.get_logs(%{})\n\n      # most-recent-first from get_logs/1; reverse to oldest-first so the\n      # stream panel reads chronologically and inspector events stack\n      # newest-first correctly.\n      rows |> Enum.take(@history_seed_cap) |> Enum.reverse()\n    else\n      []\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Normalization helpers\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  defp normalize_level(level) when is_binary(level), do: level\n  defp normalize_level(level) when is_atom(level) and not is_nil(level), do: Atom.to_string(level)\n  defp normalize_level(_), do: \"info\"\n\n  defp normalize_route(nil), do: nil\n  defp normalize_route(\"\"), do: nil\n  defp normalize_route(route) when is_binary(route), do: route\n  defp normalize_route(route), do: to_string(route)\n\n  defp ui_level(\"debug\"), do: \"DEBUG\"\n  defp ui_level(\"warning\"), do: \"WARN\"\n  defp ui_level(\"error\"), do: \"ERROR\"\n  defp ui_level(_), do: \"INFO\"\n\n  defp truncate(nil), do: \"\"\n\n  defp truncate(message) when is_binary(message) do\n    if String.length(message) > 120, do: String.slice(message, 0, 120) <> \"…\", else: message\n  end\n\n  defp truncate(message), do: truncate(to_string(message))\n\n  # Live broadcasts carry no timestamp -> \"now\"; mount-time LogStore history\n  # rows carry `inserted_at` (DateTime/NaiveDateTime) -> converted so the\n  # 60-second window judges seeded rows by their REAL age, not by mount time.\n  defp entry_timestamp(entry) do\n    case entry[:inserted_at] do\n      %DateTime{} = dt ->\n        {DateTime.to_unix(dt, :millisecond), Calendar.strftime(dt, \"%H:%M:%S\")}\n\n      %NaiveDateTime{} = naive ->\n        dt = DateTime.from_naive!(naive, \"Etc/UTC\")\n        {DateTime.to_unix(dt, :millisecond), Calendar.strftime(dt, \"%H:%M:%S\")}\n\n      _ ->\n        now = DateTime.utc_now()\n        {DateTime.to_unix(now, :millisecond), Calendar.strftime(now, \"%H:%M:%S\")}\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Render\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n  def render(assigns) do\n    groups = domain_groups(assigns.endpoint_order, assigns.extra_domains)\n\n    layout =\n      TraceMapGeometry.layout(\n        Enum.map(groups, fn {domain, leaves} -> %{key: domain, satellites: leaves} end)\n      )\n\n    assigns =\n      assigns\n      |> assign(:nodes, build_nodes(groups, layout, assigns))\n      |> assign(:edges, build_edges(groups, layout))\n      |> assign(:visible_lines, visible_lines(assigns))\n      |> assign(:inspected_node, inspected_node(assigns))\n      |> assign(:ui_levels, @ui_levels)\n\n    ~H\"\"\"\n    <div\n      id=\"trace-map-leitstand\"\n      phx-hook=\"TraceMapLeitstand\"\n      class=\"flex flex-col gap-3\"\n      style=\"height: calc(100vh - 6.5rem);\"\n    >\n      <div class=\"flex items-center justify-between shrink-0\">\n        <h1 class=\"text-heading font-semibold\">Trace Map</h1>\n      </div>\n\n      <%!-- AC6: war-room banner — always present in markup, toggled via the\n            boolean `hidden` attribute; role=status/aria-live=polite so an\n            admin starting a session while this page is open gets announced. --%>\n      <%!-- No `flex` utility here, deliberately: an author-stylesheet\n            `.flex { display: flex }` beats the UA's `[hidden] { display: none }`\n            in the cascade, so the \"hidden\" banner would still render (the exact\n            trace-map-hierarchy/admin-sidebar-nesting flex-beats-hidden bug —\n            reconfirmed live in dev cycle 1). `.tm-wr-banner` owns display:flex\n            AND a `[hidden]` override in app.css. --%>\n      <div\n        data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-banner\"\n        role=\"status\"\n        aria-live=\"polite\"\n        hidden={not war_room_active?(@war_room)}\n        class=\"tm-wr-banner items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-error bg-status-error-bg px-4 py-2 text-sm shrink-0\"\n      >\n        <span data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-dot\" class=\"tm-recdot\" aria-hidden=\"true\"></span>\n        <span :if={war_room_active?(@war_room)}>\n          War Room session active — capturing\n          <b class=\"font-mono font-medium\">{war_room_session(@war_room).started_by}</b>\n          · <span\n            data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-elapsed\"\n            id=\"trace-map-war-room-elapsed\"\n            data-started-at={war_room_started_at_unix(@war_room)}\n          >{war_room_elapsed(@war_room)}</span>\n          <span :if={war_room_session(@war_room).reason} class=\"text-text-secondary\">\n            · {war_room_session(@war_room).reason}\n          </span>\n        </span>\n        <.link\n          navigate=\"/admin/logs/war-room\"\n          data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-open-link\"\n          class=\"btn btn-outline btn-error btn-xs ml-auto\"\n        >\n          Open War Room\n        </.link>\n      </div>\n\n      <div class=\"flex gap-4 flex-1 min-h-0\">\n        <div class=\"flex-1 flex flex-col min-w-0\" id=\"trace-map-left\">\n          <div class=\"relative flex-1 min-h-0 rounded-lg bg-base-400 border border-base-300\" id=\"trace-map-map-wrap\">\n            <svg\n              data-testid=\"trace-map-svg\"\n              viewBox=\"-560 -400 1120 800\"\n              preserveAspectRatio=\"xMidYMid meet\"\n              role=\"group\"\n              aria-label=\"Service health map\"\n              class=\"w-full h-full block\"\n            >\n              <g>\n                <line\n                  :for={edge <- @edges}\n                  class=\"tm-edge\"\n                  x1={edge.x1}\n                  y1={edge.y1}\n                  x2={edge.x2}\n                  y2={edge.y2}\n                />\n              </g>\n              <g id=\"trace-map-pings\"></g>\n              <g>\n                <.map_node :for={node <- @nodes} node={node} />\n              </g>\n            </svg>\n            <div data-testid=\"trace-map-tooltip\" id=\"trace-map-tooltip\" class=\"tm-tooltip\" hidden></div>\n          </div>\n\n          <%!-- AC9: resizable drag handle — pointer drag + ArrowUp/Down ±32px,\n                Home/End max/min, dblclick resets to 218px (TraceMapLeitstand hook). --%>\n          <div\n            data-testid=\"trace-map-resizer\"\n            id=\"trace-map-resizer\"\n            role=\"separator\"\n            aria-orientation=\"horizontal\"\n            tabindex=\"0\"\n            aria-label=\"Resize log stream (drag, or arrow up/down; double-click resets)\"\n            class=\"tm-resizer shrink-0\"\n          >\n          </div>\n\n          <section\n            data-testid=\"trace-map-stream\"\n            id=\"trace-map-stream\"\n            aria-label=\"Live log stream\"\n            class=\"tm-stream flex flex-col shrink-0 border border-base-300 rounded-b-lg bg-base-100\"\n          >\n            <div class=\"flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap px-3 py-2 border-b border-base-300 shrink-0\">\n              <span class=\"text-caption uppercase text-text-secondary\">Live stream</span>\n              <span role=\"group\" aria-label=\"Level filter\" class=\"inline-flex gap-1\">\n                <button\n                  :for={level <- @ui_levels}\n                  type=\"button\"\n                  data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"\n                  data-level={level}\n                  aria-pressed={to_string(MapSet.member?(@active_levels, level))}\n                  phx-click=\"toggle_level\"\n                  phx-value-level={level}\n                  class={[\n                    \"font-mono text-xs font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border\",\n                    \"focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary\",\n                    level_pill_class(level, MapSet.member?(@active_levels, level))\n                  ]}\n                >\n                  {level}\n                </button>\n              </span>\n              <span\n                :for={key <- @selected}\n                data-testid=\"trace-map-filter-chip\"\n                data-chip-value={key}\n                class=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-mono text-xs border border-primary rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 bg-base-200\"\n              >\n                {key}\n                <button\n                  type=\"button\"\n                  data-testid=\"trace-map-filter-chip-remove\"\n                  phx-click=\"toggle_node\"\n                  phx-value-key={key}\n                  aria-label={\"Remove filter \" <> key}\n                  class=\"text-primary font-bold px-0.5 focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary rounded\"\n                >\n                  ×\n                </button>\n              </span>\n              <button\n                :if={@selected != []}\n                type=\"button\"\n                data-testid=\"trace-map-clear-all\"\n                phx-click=\"clear_filters\"\n                class=\"inline-flex items-center font-mono text-xs border border-base-300 text-text-secondary rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 hover:bg-base-300 focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary\"\n              >\n                Clear all\n              </button>\n            </div>\n            <div id=\"trace-map-stream-body\" class=\"flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-3 py-1 font-mono text-xs\" tabindex=\"0\">\n              <div\n                :for={line <- @visible_lines}\n                data-testid=\"trace-map-stream-line\"\n                data-level={line.ui_level}\n                data-route={line.route || \"\"}\n                class=\"flex gap-2 whitespace-nowrap py-px\"\n              >\n                <span class=\"text-text-disabled\">{line.ts}</span>\n                <span class={[\"w-12 shrink-0 font-bold\", level_text_class(line.ui_level)]}>{line.ui_level}</span>\n                <span class=\"text-base-content\">{line.route}</span>\n                <span class=\"text-text-secondary\">{line.message}</span>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </section>\n        </div>\n\n        <%!-- AC8: sticky right column — inspector card + always-visible legend. --%>\n        <aside class=\"w-80 shrink-0 flex flex-col gap-3 overflow-y-auto\">\n          <div data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector\" class=\"rounded-lg border border-base-300 bg-base-200 p-4\">\n            <h2 class=\"text-caption uppercase text-text-secondary font-semibold mb-2\">Node inspector</h2>\n            <p :if={is_nil(@inspected_node)} data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-empty\" class=\"text-sm text-text-disabled\">\n              Hover or select a node to see its live health details.\n            </p>\n            <div :if={@inspected_node}>\n              <div data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-name\" class=\"font-semibold text-base\">\n                {@inspected_node.label}\n              </div>\n              <div data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-route\" class=\"font-mono text-xs text-text-secondary break-all mb-2\">\n                {@inspected_node.route}\n              </div>\n              <span\n                data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-status-pill\"\n                data-status={@inspected_node.status}\n                class={[\n                  \"inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold px-2.5 py-0.5 mb-3\",\n                  status_pill_class(@inspected_node.status)\n                ]}\n              >\n                {status_pill_text(@inspected_node.status)}\n              </span>\n              <div class=\"flex justify-between text-sm text-text-secondary py-0.5\">\n                <span>Requests / 60 s</span>\n                <b data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-stat-requests\" class=\"font-mono font-medium text-base-content\">\n                  {@inspected_node.counts.requests}\n                </b>\n              </div>\n              <div class=\"flex justify-between text-sm text-text-secondary py-0.5\">\n                <span>Warnings / 60 s</span>\n                <b data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-stat-warnings\" class=\"font-mono font-medium text-base-content\">\n                  {@inspected_node.counts.warnings}\n                </b>\n              </div>\n              <div class=\"flex justify-between text-sm text-text-secondary py-0.5\">\n                <span>Errors / 60 s</span>\n                <b data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-stat-errors\" class=\"font-mono font-medium text-base-content\">\n                  {@inspected_node.counts.errors}\n                </b>\n              </div>\n              <h3 class=\"text-caption uppercase text-text-secondary font-semibold mt-3 mb-1\">Recent events</h3>\n              <p :if={@inspected_node.events == []} class=\"font-mono text-xs text-text-disabled\">—</p>\n              <div\n                :for={event <- @inspected_node.events}\n                data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-event\"\n                class=\"font-mono text-xs text-text-secondary py-px\"\n              >\n                {event.ts} · {event.level} · {event.message}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n\n          <div data-testid=\"trace-map-legend\" class=\"rounded-lg border border-base-300 bg-base-200 p-4\">\n            <h2 class=\"text-caption uppercase text-text-secondary font-semibold mb-2\">Legend</h2>\n            <div\n              :for={{state, dot_class, text} <- legend_rows()}\n              data-testid=\"trace-map-legend-row\"\n              data-legend-state={state}\n              class=\"flex items-center gap-2.5 py-1 text-sm text-text-secondary\"\n            >\n              <span class={[\"tm-legend-dot\", dot_class]} aria-hidden=\"true\"></span>\n              <span>{text}</span>\n            </div>\n            <p class=\"text-xs text-text-disabled mt-2\">\n              Domains inherit the worst status of their satellites; nebu inherits the worst domain.\n              Nodes heal automatically once the 60 s window passes.\n            </p>\n          </div>\n        </aside>\n      </div>\n\n      <%!-- AC10: throttled aria-live announcer for new errors (endpoint name\n            + no message content — spec §8). --%>\n      <div data-testid=\"trace-map-announcer\" role=\"status\" aria-live=\"polite\" class=\"sr-only\">\n        {@announcement}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n    \"\"\"\n  end\n\n  # ── One SVG map node (nebu / domain / satellite) ──────────────────────────\n  #\n  # Focusable `role=\"button\"` per AC10; Enter/Space activation is wired by\n  # the TraceMapLeitstand hook (SVG <g> has no native keyboard activation).\n  # `aria-pressed` reflects selection; nebu is never a toggle target (always\n  # \"false\" — clicking it CLEARS filters instead).\n  attr(:node, :map, required: true)\n\n  defp map_node(assigns) do\n    ~H\"\"\"\n    <g\n      id={@node.dom_id}\n      data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"\n      data-node-key={@node.key}\n      data-node-kind={@node.kind}\n      data-node-status={@node.status}\n      data-node-selected={to_string(@node.selected)}\n      role=\"button\"\n      tabindex=\"0\"\n      aria-pressed={to_string(@node.pressed)}\n      aria-label={@node.aria_label}\n      data-tooltip-name={@node.label}\n      data-tooltip-route={@node.route}\n      data-tooltip-status={@node.tooltip_status}\n      data-tooltip-counts={@node.tooltip_counts}\n      data-tooltip-cta={@node.tooltip_cta}\n      transform={\"translate(#{@node.x} #{@node.y})\"}\n      phx-click={@node.click_event}\n      phx-value-key={@node.kind != \"nebu\" && @node.key}\n      class={\"tm-node tm-node--\" <> @node.status}\n    >\n      <circle class=\"tm-selring\" r={@node.r + 7} />\n      <circle data-testid=\"trace-map-node-core\" class=\"tm-node-core\" r={@node.r} />\n      <%= case @node.kind do %>\n        <% \"nebu\" -> %>\n          <text class=\"tm-nname\" y=\"-2\">nebu</text>\n          <text class=\"tm-ncount\" y=\"16\">{@node.counts.requests}/min</text>\n        <% \"domain\" -> %>\n          <text class=\"tm-nname\" y=\"-1\">{@node.label}</text>\n          <text class=\"tm-ncount\" y=\"14\">{@node.counts.requests}</text>\n        <% _satellite -> %>\n          <text class=\"tm-ncount\" y=\"4\">{@node.counts.requests}</text>\n          <text class=\"tm-nlabel\" y={@node.r + 15}>{@node.label}</text>\n      <% end %>\n      <g\n        :if={@node.status == \"warn\"}\n        data-testid=\"trace-map-badge-warn\"\n        class=\"tm-badge tm-badge--warn\"\n        transform={badge_transform(@node.r)}\n      >\n        <circle r=\"9\" />\n        <text y=\"3.5\">▲{badge_count(@node.counts.warnings)}</text>\n      </g>\n      <g\n        :if={@node.status == \"error\"}\n        data-testid=\"trace-map-badge-error\"\n        class=\"tm-badge tm-badge--error\"\n        transform={badge_transform(@node.r)}\n      >\n        <circle r=\"9\" />\n        <text y=\"3.5\">✕{badge_count(@node.counts.errors)}</text>\n      </g>\n    </g>\n    \"\"\"\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # View-model builders\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  # Groups observed endpoints by domain (REUSING TraceMapTree's canonical\n  # heuristic), then appends URL-selected domains no traffic has produced a\n  # satellite for yet.\n  defp domain_groups(endpoint_order, extra_domains) do\n    groups = TraceMapTree.group_endpoints_by_domain(endpoint_order)\n    known = Enum.map(groups, fn {domain, _leaves} -> domain end)\n\n    groups ++ (extra_domains |> Enum.reject(&(&1 in known)) |> Enum.map(&{&1, []}))\n  end\n\n  # DOM order (AC10 natural Tab order): nebu first, then each domain followed\n  # by its own satellites.\n  defp build_nodes(groups, layout, assigns) do\n    now = assigns.now_ms\n    states = assigns.node_states\n    selected = assigns.selected\n\n    satellite_statuses =\n      Map.new(layout.satellites, fn {key, _pos} ->\n        {key, TraceMapStatus.status(node_state(states, key), now)}\n      end)\n\n    domain_statuses =\n      Map.new(groups, fn {domain, leaves} ->\n        child_statuses = Enum.map(leaves, &Map.fetch!(satellite_statuses, &1))\n        own = TraceMapStatus.status(node_state(states, domain), now)\n        {domain, TraceMapStatus.worst([own | child_statuses])}\n      end)\n\n    nebu_status =\n      TraceMapStatus.worst([\n        TraceMapStatus.status(node_state(states, \"nebu\"), now)\n        | Map.values(domain_statuses)\n      ])\n\n    nebu_node =\n      build_node(\"nebu\", \"nebu\", layout.nebu, nebu_status, \"nebu\", \"all traffic\", selected, states, now)\n\n    domain_and_satellite_nodes =\n      Enum.flat_map(groups, fn {domain, leaves} ->\n        domain_node =\n          build_node(\n            domain,\n            \"domain\",\n            Map.fetch!(layout.domains, domain),\n            Map.fetch!(domain_statuses, domain),\n            domain_label(domain),\n            domain,\n            selected,\n            states,\n            now\n          )\n\n        satellite_nodes =\n          Enum.map(leaves, fn route ->\n            build_node(\n              route,\n              \"satellite\",\n              Map.fetch!(layout.satellites, route),\n              Map.fetch!(satellite_statuses, route),\n              satellite_label(route),\n              route,\n              selected,\n              states,\n              now\n            )\n          end)\n\n        [domain_node | satellite_nodes]\n      end)\n\n    [nebu_node | domain_and_satellite_nodes]\n  end\n\n  defp build_node(key, kind, position, status, label, route, selected, states, now) do\n    counts = TraceMapStatus.counts(node_state(states, key), now)\n    status_string = Atom.to_string(status)\n    is_selected = kind != \"nebu\" and key in selected\n\n    %{\n      key: key,\n      kind: kind,\n      # A stable DOM id per node: LiveView's DOM patching matches keyed\n      # elements in place, so a selection/status patch can never replace the\n      # <g> element itself — keyboard focus survives Enter/Space toggles\n      # (AC10; verified against the live stack in dev cycle 1).\n      dom_id: \"tm-node-\" <> Base.url_encode64(key, padding: false),\n      x: coord(position.x),\n      y: coord(position.y),\n      r: position.r,\n      status: status_string,\n      selected: is_selected,\n      pressed: is_selected,\n      label: label,\n      route: route,\n      counts: counts,\n      aria_label: node_aria_label(kind, label, route, status, counts),\n      tooltip_status: status_word(status),\n      tooltip_counts: counts_text(counts),\n      tooltip_cta: tooltip_cta(kind),\n      click_event: if(kind == \"nebu\", do: \"clear_filters\", else: \"toggle_node\")\n    }\n  end\n\n  defp node_state(states, key), do: Map.get(states, key, TraceMapStatus.new())\n\n  defp build_edges(groups, layout) do\n    domain_edges =\n      Enum.map(layout.domains, fn {_key, domain} ->\n        edge(layout.nebu, domain)\n      end)\n\n    satellite_edges =\n      Enum.flat_map(groups, fn {domain, leaves} ->\n        domain_pos = Map.fetch!(layout.domains, domain)\n        Enum.map(leaves, fn route -> edge(domain_pos, Map.fetch!(layout.satellites, route)) end)\n      end)\n\n    domain_edges ++ satellite_edges\n  end\n\n  defp edge(a, b) do\n    {x1, y1, x2, y2} = TraceMapGeometry.trim_edge(a, b)\n    %{x1: coord(x1), y1: coord(y1), x2: coord(x2), y2: coord(y2)}\n  end\n\n  defp coord(value), do: :erlang.float_to_binary(value / 1, decimals: 2)\n\n  defp visible_lines(assigns) do\n    Enum.filter(assigns.stream_entries, fn line ->\n      MapSet.member?(assigns.active_levels, line.ui_level) and\n        endpoint_match?(line, assigns.selected)\n    end)\n  end\n\n  # AC5: OR within the endpoint dimension; a selected DOMAIN matches all its\n  # satellites' lines.\n  defp endpoint_match?(_line, []), do: true\n\n  defp endpoint_match?(line, selected) do\n    line.route in selected or (line.domain != nil and line.domain in selected)\n  end\n\n  defp inspected_node(%{inspected: nil}), do: nil\n\n  defp inspected_node(assigns) do\n    key = assigns.inspected\n    now = assigns.now_ms\n    state = node_state(assigns.node_states, key)\n\n    {label, route} =\n      cond do\n        key == \"nebu\" -> {\"nebu\", \"all traffic\"}\n        TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(key) == key -> {domain_label(key), key}\n        true -> {satellite_label(key), key}\n      end\n\n    %{\n      label: label,\n      route: route,\n      status: state |> TraceMapStatus.status(now) |> Atom.to_string(),\n      counts: TraceMapStatus.counts(state, now),\n      events: Map.get(assigns.node_events, key, [])\n    }\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Labels / text helpers\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  defp domain_label(domain), do: Map.get(@domain_labels, domain, domain)\n\n  defp satellite_label(route) do\n    route\n    |> String.split(\"/\")\n    |> Enum.reject(&(&1 == \"\"))\n    |> List.last()\n    |> case do\n      nil -> route\n      segment -> segment\n    end\n  end\n\n  # AC10: name + route + status + counts, e.g.\n  # \"users endpoint · /admin/users · errors · 1 error · 0 warnings · 1 request in the last 60 seconds\"\n  defp node_aria_label(kind, label, route, status, counts) do\n    name =\n      case kind do\n        \"nebu\" -> \"nebu\"\n        \"domain\" -> label <> \" domain\"\n        _ -> label <> \" endpoint\"\n      end\n\n    Enum.join(\n      [\n        name,\n        route,\n        status_word(status),\n        pluralize(counts.errors, \"error\"),\n        pluralize(counts.warnings, \"warning\"),\n        pluralize(counts.requests, \"request\") <> \" in the last 60 seconds\"\n      ],\n      \" · \"\n    )\n  end\n\n  defp status_word(:ok), do: \"healthy\"\n  defp status_word(:warn), do: \"warnings\"\n  defp status_word(:error), do: \"errors\"\n\n  defp counts_text(counts) do\n    \"#{pluralize(counts.requests, \"request\")} · #{pluralize(counts.warnings, \"warning\")} · \" <>\n      \"#{pluralize(counts.errors, \"error\")} in the last 60s\"\n  end\n\n  defp pluralize(1, word), do: \"1 \" <> word\n  defp pluralize(n, word), do: \"#{n} #{word}s\"\n\n  defp tooltip_cta(\"nebu\"), do: \"Click to clear all filters\"\n  defp tooltip_cta(_kind), do: \"Click to filter the stream\"\n\n  defp badge_count(count) when count > 9, do: \"9+\"\n  defp badge_count(count), do: Integer.to_string(count)\n\n  defp badge_transform(radius) do\n    offset = coord(radius * 0.72)\n    \"translate(#{offset} -#{offset})\"\n  end\n\n  defp legend_rows do\n    [\n      {\"ok\", \"tm-legend-dot--ok\", \"Healthy — recedes into the background\"},\n      {\"warn\", \"tm-legend-dot--warn\", \"▲ Warnings in the last 60 s\"},\n      {\"error\", \"tm-legend-dot--error\", \"✕ Errors in the last 60 s — pulses\"},\n      {\"selected\", \"tm-legend-dot--selected\", \"Selected as stream filter\"},\n      {\"off\", \"tm-legend-dot--off\",\n       \"Long-polling endpoint — logging off by default; click to capture for 5 min\"},\n      {\"temp\", \"tm-legend-dot--temp\", \"Dashed = temporary capture, falls back to off automatically\"}\n    ]\n  end\n\n  defp level_pill_class(\"DEBUG\", true), do: \"text-text-secondary border-text-secondary\"\n  defp level_pill_class(\"INFO\", true), do: \"text-base-content border-text-secondary\"\n  defp level_pill_class(\"WARN\", true), do: \"text-warning border-warning bg-status-warn-bg\"\n  defp level_pill_class(\"ERROR\", true), do: \"text-error border-error bg-status-error-bg\"\n  defp level_pill_class(_level, false), do: \"text-text-disabled border-base-300\"\n\n  defp level_text_class(\"ERROR\"), do: \"text-error\"\n  defp level_text_class(\"WARN\"), do: \"text-warning\"\n  defp level_text_class(\"DEBUG\"), do: \"text-text-disabled\"\n  defp level_text_class(_), do: \"text-text-secondary\"\n\n  defp status_pill_class(\"ok\"), do: \"bg-status-ok-bg text-success\"\n  defp status_pill_class(\"warn\"), do: \"bg-status-warn-bg text-warning\"\n  defp status_pill_class(\"error\"), do: \"bg-status-error-bg text-error\"\n\n  defp status_pill_text(\"ok\"), do: \"● Healthy\"\n  defp status_pill_text(\"warn\"), do: \"▲ Warnings\"\n  defp status_pill_text(\"error\"), do: \"✕ Errors\"\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # War-room banner helpers (AC6)\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  defp war_room_active?({:ok, _session}), do: true\n  defp war_room_active?(_), do: false\n\n  # Only ever called inside `:if={war_room_active?(@war_room)}` blocks, so a\n  # fallback clause would be dead code (flagged by --warnings-as-errors).\n  defp war_room_session({:ok, session}), do: session\n\n  defp war_room_started_at_unix({:ok, %{started_at: %DateTime{} = started_at}}) do\n    Integer.to_string(DateTime.to_unix(started_at))\n  end\n\n  defp war_room_started_at_unix(_), do: nil\n\n  defp war_room_elapsed({:ok, %{started_at: %DateTime{} = started_at}}) do\n    total = max(DateTime.diff(DateTime.utc_now(), started_at, :second), 0)\n    format_clock(total)\n  end\n\n  defp war_room_elapsed(_), do: \"00:00\"\n\n  defp format_clock(total_seconds) when total_seconds >= 3600 do\n    hours = div(total_seconds, 3600)\n    rest = rem(total_seconds, 3600)\n    \"#{pad2(hours)}:#{pad2(div(rest, 60))}:#{pad2(rem(rest, 60))}\"\n  end\n\n  defp format_clock(total_seconds) do\n    \"#{pad2(div(total_seconds, 60))}:#{pad2(rem(total_seconds, 60))}\"\n  end\n\n  defp pad2(n), do: String.pad_leading(Integer.to_string(n), 2, \"0\")\nend\n","numLines":1081,"startLine":1,"totalLines":1081}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"7befd1dd-b9c0-4b93-a13c-07d2432beb36","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

Self-hosted WOFF2 fonts (no CDN — data sovereignty).\n */\n\n@font-face {\n  font-family: \"Inter\";\n  font-style: normal;\n  font-weight: 400;\n  font-display: swap;\n  src: url(\"/admin/static/fonts/Inter-Regular.woff2\") format(\"woff2\");\n}\n@font-face {\n  font-family: \"Inter\";\n  font-style: normal;\n  font-weight: 500;\n  font-display: swap;\n  src: url(\"/admin/static/fonts/Inter-Medium.woff2\") format(\"woff2\");\n}\n@font-face {\n  font-family: \"Inter\";\n  font-style: normal;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  font-display: swap;\n  src: url(\"/admin/static/fonts/Inter-SemiBold.woff2\") format(\"woff2\");\n}\n@font-face {\n  font-family: \"JetBrains Mono\";\n  font-style: normal;\n  font-weight: 400;\n  font-display: swap;\n  src: url(\"/admin/static/fonts/JetBrainsMono-Regular.woff2\") format(\"woff2\");\n}\n\n@tailwind base;\n@tailwind components;\n@tailwind utilities;\n\n/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n * Trace Map \"Leitstand\" (enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand)\n *\n * Visual states per the design spec §2 / trace-map-leitstand-dummy.html,\n * using the Obsidian theme's own color values (tailwind.config.js).\n * Status colors are reserved for health; primary orange is selection/focus\n * ONLY. All animations are pure CSS and disabled under\n * prefers-reduced-motion (AC10 — blocking accessibility requirement).\n * ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n.tm-edge {\n  stroke: #374151; /* --line / base-300 */\n  stroke-width: 1;\n  opacity: 0.55;\n}\n\n.tm-node {\n  cursor: pointer;\n}\n\n.tm-node-core {\n  transition: stroke 0.25s, fill 0.25s, fill-opacity 0.25s, stroke-opacity 0.25s;\n}\n\n/* ok: healthy recedes — dim fill, soft green stroke, dimmed label */\n.tm-node--ok .tm-node-core {\n  fill: #1f2937; /* base-200 */\n  fill-opacity: 0.35;\n  stroke: #22c55e; /* success */\n  stroke-opacity: 0.55;\n  stroke-width: 1.5;\n}\n\n/* warn: amber stroke + soft glow + ▲ badge (badge markup is server-side) */\n.tm-node--warn .tm-node-core {\n  fill: #431407; /* status-warn-bg */\n  fill-opacity: 0.9;\n  stroke: #f59e0b; /* warning */\n  stroke-width: 2.5;\n  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.45));\n}\n\n/* error: red stroke + glow + pulse (pulse disabled under reduced motion) */\n.tm-node--error .tm-node-core {\n  fill: #450a0a; /* status-error-bg */\n  fill-opacity: 0.95;\n  stroke: #ef4444; /* error */\n  stroke-width: 3;\n  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 10px rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.6));\n  animation: tm-errpulse 1.4s ease-in-out infinite;\n}\n\n@keyframes tm-errpulse {\n  0%,\n  100% {\n    stroke-opacity: 1;\n    filter: drop-shadow(0 0 10px rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.6));\n  }\n  50% {\n    stroke-opacity: 0.5;\n    filter: drop-shadow(0 0 3px rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.25));\n  }\n}\n\n/* Node labels/counters (mono counters per spec §2) */\n.tm-nname {\n  font-family: Inter, system-ui, sans-serif;\n  font-size: 13px;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  text-anchor: middle;\n  fill: #f9fafb;\n  pointer-events: none;\n}\n\n.tm-nlabel {\n  font-family: Inter, system-ui, sans-serif;\n  font-size: 12px;\n  text-anchor: middle;\n  fill: #9ca3af;\n  pointer-events: none;\n}\n\n.tm-ncount {\n  font-family: \"JetBrains Mono\", ui-monospace, monospace;\n  font-size: 11.5px;\n  text-anchor: middle;\n  fill: #9ca3af;\n  pointer-events: none;\n}\n\n/* ok recedes: dimmed label (status is never color-alone — AC4) */\n.tm-node--ok .tm-nlabel {\n  opacity: 0.75;\n}\n\n.tm-node--warn .tm-nlabel,\n.tm-node--error .tm-nlabel,\n.tm-node--warn .tm-ncount,\n.tm-node--error .tm-ncount {\n  fill: #f9fafb;\n  opacity: 1;\n}\n\n/* Selection ring — primary orange ONLY, never used for health (AC4) */\n.tm-selring {\n  fill: none;\n  stroke: #f97316; /* primary */\n  stroke-width: 2.5;\n  display: none;\n}\n\n.tm-node[data-node-selected=\"true\"] .tm-selring {\n  display: block;\n}\n\n/* AC10: visible focus ring on every node (never removed) */\n[data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"]:focus-visible {\n  outline: 2px solid #f97316;\n  outline-offset: 2px;\n}\n\n/* Status badges (▲/✕ + count) — status is never conveyed by color alone */\n.tm-badge {\n  pointer-events: none;\n}\n\n.tm-badge circle {\n  stroke: #0d1117; /* base-400 map background */\n  stroke-width: 1.5;\n}\n\n.tm-badge--warn circle {\n  fill: #f59e0b;\n}\n\n.tm-badge--error circle {\n  fill: #ef4444;\n}\n\n.tm-badge text {\n  font-family: \"JetBrains Mono\", ui-monospace, monospace;\n  font-size: 8px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  fill: #1c1917;\n  text-anchor: middle;\n}\n\n.tm-badge--error text {\n  fill: #ffffff;\n}\n\n/* Decorative event pings (created by the TraceMapLeitstand hook on status\n * transitions; the animation itself is pure CSS — AC11) */\n.tm-ping {\n  fill: none;\n  stroke-width: 2;\n  animation: tm-ping 0.7s ease-out forwards;\n  pointer-events: none;\n  transform-box: fill-box;\n  transform-origin: center;\n}\n\n.tm-ping--warn {\n  stroke: #f59e0b;\n}\n\n.tm-ping--error {\n  stroke: #ef4444;\n}\n\n@keyframes tm-ping {\n  from {\n    opacity: 0.55;\n  }\n  to {\n    opacity: 0;\n    transform: scale(1.55);\n  }\n}\n\n/* War-room banner recording dot (AC6) */\n.tm-recdot {\n  width: 8px;\n  height: 8px;\n  border-radius: 50%;\n  background: #ef4444;\n  flex-shrink: 0;\n  animation: tm-recblink 1.2s ease-in-out infinite;\n}\n\n@keyframes tm-recblink {\n  0%,\n  100% {\n    opacity: 1;\n  }\n  50% {\n    opacity: 0.25;\n  }\n}\n\n/* Tooltip (AC7 — positioned by the hook, content from data-tooltip-*) */\n.tm-tooltip {\n  position: absolute;\n  z-index: 20;\n  pointer-events: none;\n  background: #1f2937;\n  border: 1px solid #374151;\n  border-radius: 8px;\n  padding: 10px 12px;\n  font-size: 12.5px;\n  min-width: 200px;\n  max-width: 260px;\n  box-shadow: 0 8px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5);\n}\n\n.tm-tooltip[hidden] {\n  display: none;\n}\n\n.tm-tooltip-name {\n  font-weight: 600;\n  font-size: 13px;\n}\n\n.tm-tooltip-route {\n  font-family: \"JetBrains Mono\", ui-monospace, monospace;\n  font-size: 11.5px;\n  color: #9ca3af;\n  margin-bottom: 6px;\n  word-break: break-all;\n}\n\n.tm-tooltip-status,\n.tm-tooltip-counts {\n  color: #9ca3af;\n}\n\n.tm-tooltip-cta {\n  margin-top: 6px;\n  color: #f97316;\n  font-size: 11.5px;\n}\n\n/* Resizer (AC9) — visible affordance bar; orange on hover/focus */\n.tm-resizer {\n  height: 8px;\n  cursor: row-resize;\n  background: #111827;\n  border-top: 1px solid #374151;\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  touch-action: none;\n}\n\n.tm-resizer::before {\n  content: \"\";\n  width: 44px;\n  height: 3px;\n  border-radius: 2px;\n  background: #374151;\n}\n\n.tm-resizer:hover::before,\n.tm-resizer:focus-visible::before {\n  background: #f97316;\n}\n\n.tm-resizer:focus-visible {\n  outline: 2px solid #f97316;\n  outline-offset: -2px;\n}\n\n/* Stream panel default height (dblclick on the resizer resets to this) */\n.tm-stream {\n  height: 218px;\n}\n\n/* Legend dots (AC8 — one per visual state, mirrors the node styling) */\n.tm-legend-dot {\n  width: 18px;\n  height: 18px;\n  border-radius: 50%;\n  flex-shrink: 0;\n  display: inline-block;\n}\n\n.tm-legend-dot--ok {\n  background: rgba(31, 41, 55, 0.35);\n  border: 1.5px solid rgba(34, 197, 94, 0.55);\n}\n\n.tm-legend-dot--warn {\n  background: #431407;\n  border: 2.5px solid #f59e0b;\n}\n\n.tm-legend-dot--error {\n  background: #450a0a;\n  border: 3px solid #ef4444;\n}\n\n.tm-legend-dot--selected {\n  background: transparent;\n  border: 2.5px solid #f97316;\n}\n\n.tm-legend-dot--off {\n  background: transparent;\n  border: 1.5px dashed #4b5563;\n}\n\n.tm-legend-dot--temp {\n  background: rgba(31, 41, 55, 0.35);\n  border: 2px dashed #22c55e;\n}\n\n/* AC10 (blocking): reduced motion disables ALL decorative animation —\n * error pulse, event pings, and the recording dot's blink. */\n@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {\n  .tm-node--error .tm-node-core {\n    animation: none;\n  }\n\n  .tm-ping {\n    display: none;\n  }\n\n  .tm-recdot {\n    animation: none;\n  }\n}\n\n/* War-room banner (AC6): owns its display so the boolean `hidden` attribute\n * actually hides it — a bare Tailwind `.flex` utility would beat the UA's\n * `[hidden] { display: none }` rule in the cascade (the known\n * flex-beats-hidden trap; reconfirmed against the live stack, dev cycle 1). */\n.tm-wr-banner {\n  display: flex;\n}\n\n.tm-wr-banner[hidden] {\n  display: none;\n}\n","numLines":385,"startLine":1,"totalLines":385}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f5edaad5-1109-4f4e-9b9f-8f14648ab3c0","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}
