demo controls are clearly badged.\n-->\n<style>\n  :root {\n    --bg:        #0d1117;\n    --surface:   #111827;\n    --panel:     #1f2937;\n    --line:      #374151;\n    --text:      #f9fafb;\n    --text-2:    #9ca3af;\n    --text-3:    #4b5563;\n    --primary:   #f97316;\n    --primary-h: #ea580c;\n    --ok:        #22c55e;\n    --warn:      #f59e0b;\n    --err:       #ef4444;\n    --ok-bg:     #052e16;\n    --warn-bg:   #431407;\n    --err-bg:    #450a0a;\n    --sans: Inter, system-ui, -apple-system, \"Segoe UI\", sans-serif;\n    --mono: \"JetBrains Mono\", ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, monospace;\n  }\n  * { box-sizing: border-box; }\n  html, body { height: 100%; }\n  body {\n    margin: 0; background: var(--surface); color: var(--text);\n    font-family: var(--sans); font-size: 15px; line-height: 1.5;\n    overflow: hidden;\n  }\n  button { font-family: var(--sans); }\n\n  /* ── Shell ─────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n  .shell { display: flex; flex-direction: column; height: 100vh; }\n  .topbar {\n    height: 48px; background: var(--line); flex-shrink: 0;\n    display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between;\n    padding: 0 16px; gap: 12px;\n  }\n  .topbar .brand { font-weight: 600; }\n  .topbar-pills { display: flex; gap: 8px; align-items: center; }\n  .pill {\n    display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px;\n    font-size: 12px; letter-spacing: .06em; text-transform: uppercase;\n    padding: 3px 10px; border-radius: 999px; border: 1px solid var(--text-3);\n    color: var(--text-2); background: var(--surface);\n  }\n  .pill--warroom { border-color: var(--err); color: #fca5a5; display: none; }\n  .pill--warroom.on { display: inline-flex; }\n  .recdot {\n    width: 8px; height: 8px; border-radius: 50%; background: var(--err);\n    animation: recblink 1.2s ease-in-out infinite;\n  }\n  @keyframes recblink { 0%,100% { opacity: 1 } 50% { opacity: .25 } }\n  .view.active { display: flex; }\n\n  /* ── Trace map view layout ─────────────────────────────── */\n  .tm { flex-direction: column; }\n  .tm-toolbar {\n    display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; flex-wrap: wrap;\n    padding: 10px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); background: var(--surface);\n  }\n  .tm-toolbar h1 { font-size: 16px; font-weight: 600; margin: 0 8px 0 0; letter-spacing: -0.01em; }\n  .badge-demo {\n    font-size: 11px; letter-spacing: .08em; text-transform: uppercase;\n    color: var(--warn); border: 1px dashed var(--warn); border-radius: 4px;\n    padding: 2px 8px;\n  }\n  .btn {\n    background: var(--panel); color: var(--text); border: 1px solid var(--line);\n    border-radius: 6px; padding: 5px 12px; font-size: 13px; cursor: pointer;\n  }\n  .btn:hover { background: var(--line); }\n  .btn--warn  { border-color: var(--warn); color: var(--warn); }\n  .btn--err   { border-color: var(--err);  color: #fca5a5; }\n  .btn--primary { background: var(--primary); border-color: var(--primary); color: #1c1917; font-weight: 600; }\n  .btn--primary:hover { background: var(--primary-h); }\n  .rate { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-2); }\n  .rate input { accent-color: var(--primary); width: 90px; }\n\n  .tm-main { display: flex; flex: 1; min-height: 0; }\n  .tm-left { flex: 1; display: flex; flex-direction: column; min-width: 0; }\n  .tm-map { flex: 1; min-height: 0; background: var(--bg); position: relative; }\n  .tm-map svg { width: 100%; height: 100%; display: block; }\n\n  /* ── Map nodes ─────────────────────────────────────────── */\n  .edge { stroke: var(--line); stroke-width: 1; opacity: .55; }\n  .node { cursor: pointer; }\n  .node:focus { outline: none; }\n  .node .core { transition: stroke .25s, fill .25s, fill-opacity .25s, stroke-opacity .25s; }\n\n  .node--ok .core   { fill: var(--panel); fill-opacity: .35; stroke: var(--ok);   stroke-opacity: .55; 0.2126*lin(r)+0.7152*lin(g)+0.0722*lin(b)\ndef ratio(fg,bg):\n    l1,l2 = sorted([lum(fg),lum(bg)],reverse=True)\n    return (l1+0.05)/(l2+0.05)\ndef blend(fg,bg,a):\n    fg=fg.lstrip('#'); bg=bg.lstrip('#')\n    return '#'+''.join(f'{round(int(fg[i:i+2],16)*a+int(bg[i:i+2],16)*(1-a)):02x}' for i in (0,2,4))\n\ncases = [\n    (\"error badge text #fff on error badge fill #ef4444\", {"parentUuid":"d62796e8-6165-4dcc-a2d0-a4875efd4dde","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c1e00925-b2e9-4f38-87d9-fd1f315ab15a","agentId":"aa2a2c647028b9c53","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CZgSUACMxy1GPM8Dc8DVMM","type":"tool_result","content":"defmodule Nebu.Admin.TraceMapGeometry do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  Pure geometry for the Trace Map \"Leitstand\" radial SVG map\n  (enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand, AC2).\n\n  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand.md\n  Design: _bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-spec.md §2\n  (the interactive dummy `trace-map-leitstand-dummy.html`'s own `MODEL`/`R1`/\n  `R2`/`rad()`/`edge()` code is the numeric ground truth this module mirrors).\n\n  A plain Elixir module — NO LiveView/HTML knowledge (spec §8 constraint 3,\n  ADR-018/019/021): `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap.render/1` renders the SVG\n  server-side in HEEx from the `{x, y}` positions computed here; LiveView\n  patches only ever update node {"parentUuid":"d5052150-348d-4e66-a38b-f89300668421","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"aa2a2c647028b9c53","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5","id":"msg_011Cco5UEmLDzkgg8tsgSuk5","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01RVn54pq8DBYwmwus9xtxNM","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cd /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-philippbeyerlein-dev-01-gitlab-1200-philippb-open-chat-scripts/84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82/scratchpad && cat > svgfocus.html <<'EOF'\n<!doctype html><meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n<style>\n  body { background:#0d1117; margin:0; }\n  [data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"]:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid #f97316; outline-offset: 2px; }\n</style>\n<svg viewBox=\"-100 -100 200 200\" width=\"400\" height=\"400\">\n  <g data-testid=\"trace-map-node\" role=\"button\" tabindex=\"0\">\n    <circle r=\"24\" fill=\"#1f2937\" stroke=\"#22c55e\" stroke-width=\"1.5\"/>\n  </g>\n</svg>\nEOF\ncat > svgfocus.mjs <<'EOF'\nimport { chromium } from '/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/e2e/node_modules/playwright/index.mjs';\nconst browser = await chromium.launch();\nconst page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 400, height: 400 } });\nawait page.goto('file://' + process.cwd() + '/svgfocus.html');\nconst before = await page.screenshot();\nawait page.keyboard.press('Tab'); Audit complete.\n\n---\n\n# UX Gate (Step 5b) — Accessibility & Design-Consistency Audit: `trace-map-leitstand`\n\n**Verdict: 5 violations, 8 advisories**\n\nScope audited: `/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map.ex`, `server/assets/css/app.css`, `server/priv/static/assets/app.js`, `server/lib/nebu/admin/layouts.ex`, `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live.ex`, geometry/status modules — against story AC4/AC10 (hard gates), spec §2/§3/§6/§7, the approved dummy, WCAG 2.1 AA, and Obsidian-theme consistency. Contrast ratios below were computed exactly (WCAG relative-luminance formula), including alpha-blended fills.\n\n## Violations (blocking)\n\n**V1 — War-room banner is a live region containing a per-second ticking clock** — WCAG 4.1.3 / aria-live misuse; AC6+AC10.\n`server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map.ex:491-518`: the entire banner is `role=\"status\" aria-live=\"polite\"`, and inside it sits `#trace-map-war-room-elapsed`, whose text the hook rewrites every 1 s (`server/priv/static/assets/app.js:1329-1331`, `traceMapTickWarRoomClock` at 1436-1453) and the server additionally re-renders on the 2 s war-room poll. `role=\"status\"` has implicit `aria-atomic=\"true\"`, so a screen reader re-announces the full banner sentence every second for the entire session. Fix: make the banner itself non-live and announce session start/stop through a dedicated (or the existing sr-only) live region containing only the state-change sentence; at minimum, scope the live region so the clock is outside it.\n\n**V2 — Error badge count text fails contrast: white on `#ef4444` = 3.76:1** (< 4.5:1, WCAG 1.4.3; AC10 \"all text meets 4.5:1\").\n`server/assets/css/app.css:188-190` (`.tm-badge--error text { fill: #ffffff }`); markup at `trace_map.ex:768-776`. The theme's own token pair prescribes the fix: `error-content: #000000` (\"black on red — 5.9:1\", `server/assets/tailwind.config.js:32`) → measured 5.58:1. The warn badge already does it right (`#1c1917` on amber = 8.14:1). Note: the dummy has the same flaw (`.sbadge--err text { fill:#fff }`) — AC10 overrides the dummy; fix both.\n\n**V3 — `text-text-disabled` (#4b5563) used as text color for operable/informative content** — WCAG 1.4.3; also a consistency break with the sibling Live Stream page.\n- Inactive level pills (`trace_map.ex:1030`): 2.35:1. These are enabled toggle buttons (`aria-pressed=\"false\"`), not disabled controls — the \"inactive UI component\" exemption does not apply. Matches the dummy's `--text-3`, but AC10 is the hard gate; `text-text-secondary` (#9ca3af, 6.99:1) still reads as clearly dimmed against the colored active pills.\n- Stream-line timestamps (`trace_map.ex:624`) and DEBUG level text (`level_text_class/1`, line 1034): 2.35:1. The Live Stream page renders the same data in `text-text-secondary` (`logs_live.ex:1128`) — `text-text-disabled` as running text appears nowhere else in the admin UI.\n- Inspector empty-state instruction (line 637), empty-events dash (line 676), and the legend's worst-child-propagation note (lines 698-701) on the `base-200` card: **1.94:1** — the lowest ratio on the page, on the text that explains how the map works.\n\n**V4 — Focusable `role=\"separator\"` resizer has no `aria-valuenow`** — WCAG 4.1.2 (Name, Role, Value); ARIA 1.2 requires `aria-valuenow` on focusable separators (window-splitter pattern).\n`trace_map.ex:551-560` has role/orientation/tabindex/label but no value attributes, and the hook's `setStreamHeight` (`app.js:1278-1282`) never sets one — a screen-reader user pressing ArrowUp/Down/Home/End (`app.js:1302-1319`) gets zero feedback on position. Fix: maintain `aria-valuenow` (e.g. stream height as percent, with `aria-valuemin=\"0\"`/`aria-valuemax=\"100\"`) inside `setStreamHeight`.\n\n**V5 — `text-error` (#ef4444) on `bg-status-error-bg` (#450a0a) = 4.29:1** — marginal 1.4.3 fail and a deviation from the dummy, which uses `#fca5a5` on the same background (8.51:1) for exactly this pairing.\nAffects: active ERROR level pill (`trace_map.ex:1029`), inspector error status pill (`status_pill_class(\"error\")`, line 1039), and the `btn-outline btn-error` \"Open War Room\" link on the banner's error background (line 514). WARN's equivalent pairing passes (7.29:1). Fix: adopt the dummy's `#fca5a5` (e.g. a `status-error-text` token) for text on `status-error-bg`.\n\n## Advisories (non-blocking)\n\n**A1 — Focus/hover does not target the inspector.** Spec §3: \"Focus also targets the node in the inspector\"; AC8 says \"selected/focused\", and the empty-state copy itself promises \"Hover or select a node…\". `@inspected` is only set in `toggle_node` (`trace_map.ex:204-214`) — a user must mutate the stream filter to inspect a node. Becomes more important in `trace-map-node-controls`; suggest a focus/hover-driven event now or amend the empty-state copy.\n\n**A2 — Error announcer drops the count and can't re-announce repeats.** `trace_map.ex:368-377` renders \"New errors detected in <route>\" (AC10 example: \"3 new errors detected in sync endpoint\"). Because the announcement string only changes when the route changes, a second error burst on the same endpoint after the debounce produces no DOM change and thus no announcement. Including the count fixes both.\n\n**A3 — nebu carries a permanent `aria-pressed=\"false\"`** (`build_node/9`, `trace_map.ex:862/876`) although it is a clear-all action, not a toggle — announced as \"toggle button, not pressed\". Suggest omitting `aria-pressed` on the center node.\n\n**A4 — `focus:outline-none` + `focus-visible:ring` on pills/chips/clear-all** (`trace_map.ex:581, 601, 611`). Visible in normal rendering, but Tailwind rings are box-shadows, which forced-colors/Windows High Contrast mode strips — leaving no indicator; AC10 literally says \"no `outline: none`\". The SVG nodes and resizer already use real outlines; align the HTML controls.\n\n**A5 — Minor semantics:** the tooltip (`trace_map.ex:546`) duplicates the node `aria-label` verbatim and is not `aria-hidden=\"true\"` — virtual-cursor users encounter the content twice; and `#trace-map-stream-body` (line 616) is a `tabindex=\"0\"` scroll container with no role/name — `role=\"log\"` or an `aria-label` would name it.\n\n**A6 — Focus-ring mechanism deviates from AC10's prescribed inner `<circle class=\"ring\">`:** implementation uses CSS `outline` on the `<g>` (`app.css:157-160`). Verified painting under keyboard focus in Chromium during this audit, and it passes non-text contrast (6.75:1 on the map bg, 5.24:1 on cards) — acceptable, but outlines on SVG elements only paint in Safari ≥ 16.4; worth one cross-browser sanity check.\n\n**A7 — Badge text is 8px vs the dummy's 9.5px** (`app.css:182`) — makes the count harder to read; bump alongside the V2 fix.\n\n**A8 — Home/End direction:** implementation and the executable feature file pin Home = minimize stream / End = maximize, while AC9 prose says the opposite (already flagged by dev). Concur with following the test-reviewed feature file; AC9's prose should be amended so the contradiction doesn't resurface.\n\n## Verified clean\n\n- **AC4 visual states / design fidelity:** geometry constants (r 52/38/24, R1 195, R2 128, fan `min(130°, n·26°)`, angles −48/48/132/−132, viewBox) match spec §2 and the dummy's model exactly; ok/warn/error fills, strokes, glows and the 1.4 s pulse match; selection ring is primary-orange-only at r+7/2.5px, never health; ▲/✕ badges with counts, dimmed ok labels, and status words in every `aria-label` provide the non-color redundancy (the 55%-opacity green ok stroke is fine on that basis).\n- **Legend** enumerates all 6 states including the two deferred ones, with the propagation note (`trace_map.ex:1014-1024`).\n- **flex-beats-hidden:** banner fixed via `.tm-wr-banner` + `[hidden]` override — present in the rebuilt `server/priv/static/assets/app.css`; tooltip has its own `[hidden]` override; no other hidden+display-utility combination exists in the changed templates.\n- **prefers-reduced-motion** disables error pulse, pings, and the recording-dot blink (`app.css:360-372`), and ping spawning is also skipped in JS (`app.js:1340`).\n- **Reduced-motion/CSP:** no inline JS/handlers; single phx-hook; tooltip built with `textContent` only.\n- **Tab order** (nebu → domains → satellites per domain), Enter/Space activation, URL-driven `aria-pressed` state, throttled polite announcer region, banner copy per AC6, stream default 218 px, resizer affordance per the dummy.\n- **AC14/A1:** Logs child labeled \"Log Retention\" in `server/lib/nebu/admin/layouts.ex`, navkey unchanged.\n\nRecommendation: the five violations are all small, local fixes (one live-region restructure, three color-token swaps, one `aria-valuenow` in the existing hook) — no structural rework required.
